GBP: Taking its budget cue from gilts?
ING's Rates Strategist, Michiel Tukker, estimates that there is a 20bp
risk premium in 10-year UK gilt yields – some of which could come
out of the market during today's UK budget release. The budget
itself is not expected to have too much say on the Bank of

England's tightening prospects, although any upside surprises on

money markets.

Chris Turner
CAD: A very close call on BoC today
We think the Bank of Canada will wait until December to deliver its
ﬁrst rate hike. But speculative bets on a move today have built
materially over the past few days. The trigger was the jump in

inﬂation expectations in the 3Q BoC Business Outlook report on 19
October. Pricing for today’s meeting rose from 9bp to 20bp and
remains there as of this morning. That more than oﬀset a softish
September CPI print, with headline inﬂation rising less than
expected to 3.1% and core measures staying well anchored around
2.0%.

Our call for a hold today stems from those relatively muted
inﬂation ﬁgures and softening jobs market. But we admit there is
an increasingly higher chance that policymakers will hike given

concerns about inﬂation expectations de-anchoring and seek to
avoid an adverse reaction in back-end yields, given how much is
priced in. If they do hold, expect a very hawkish statement
signalling a move in December.
Judging by the evenly split consensus, CAD faces downside risks
today given the 20bp priced in. A hike should, however, come with
indications that more tightening is coming and could send
USD/CAD back below 1.40. Beyond the near term, we don’t see

USD/CAD trading sustainably below 1.40. We think markets are
overestimating BoC tightening in 2027 and we expect lower oil
prices to leave the loonie exposed to a not-so-attractive domestic
backdrop. A return to 1.420 remains a risk in our view.
Francesco Pesole

### Page 1

Equity Research 
Sector Report | Technology｜Oct 15, 2026 
Oct Edition: Choppy Markets, Intact AI

Overseas  
Technology

Jeff Pu, CFA 
SFC CE No. BNO719 
jeffpu@gfgroup.com.hk 
Henry Huang 
SFC CE No. BYA749 
henryhuang@gfgroup.com.hk

Market to remain choppy: Tech equities were volatile in the past month, with rotation 
toward hyperscaler and internet names. That said , s emi and memory (Korea) 
weakness reflected lower risk appetite due to rising bond yields, AI monetization/ARR 
visibility and L-T AI debates (i.e., Broadcom CEO warned that capital may now be a 
bigger constraint), even as company-specific catalysts continued to be strong. On the 
other hand, the positive was the acceleration of AI model scaling (i.e., Meta’s Muse 
Wonder, Gemini 4, etc). That said, w e view the pullback primarily as a reset in 
expectations rather than the evidence of AI deterioration. Despite light positioning, we 
expect the markets will remain choppy in the near term but would  accumulate low 
valuation & sustainable names  (i.e., HBM, non-TSMC foundries, CPUs, CPOs, etc) . 
In our preferred list, we added CLS, while removing MTK and Hon Hai.  
U.S. roadshow takeaways (week of Oct 5): Investor tone skewed neutral to slightly 
cautious, driven by conservative top ‑down macro views, U.S. election, and ongoing 
debates around the L-T AI demand. Positioning in AI infrastructure has come off peak 
levels. Even  so, many investors would accumulate on pullbacks, with interest 
concentrated in “Made in U.S.” names (INTC, LITE, COHR), U.S. memory suppliers, 
TSMC, and selective bottleneck themes — including ASE, ABF substrates, 
MRVL/SMTC, and Japanese MLCCs. 
OCP & earnings season in focus: The week is anchored by AI/semis — SEMICON 
West, OCP, and earnings from ASML, TSMC, etc. OCP reinforced the industry’s pivot 
toward rack‑scale infrastructure, with inference and agentic workloads driving demand 
across compute, memory, and networking. Scale‑up optics, NPO/CPO, and 800VDC 
power were key themes, while AMD’s CPU outlook and Helios/Helion underscored a 
secular CPU uptrend. Nvidia ’s use of PTFE for NVLink boards further supports our 
positive stance on PTFE (see PCB Spec in 2 H27, Oct 5).  On earnings, ASML 
delivered largely in -line results, GM and OP above consensus with revenue slightly 
miss, and raised full year guidance again and is exploring ways to increase capacity 
by >30% 2028 to fulfill strong customer demand, also extending the order visibility 
beyond 2028. For TSMC, we anticipate an inline quarter. 
September monthly sales/data: TSMC sales -0.6% MoM, slightly ahead. Rubin to 
accelerate and T3 ramp. PC forecast trimmed. Substrate/CCL upcycle with 
pricing/margin expansion. Aspeed revenue +101% YoY. Details on page 5. 
GFHK Tech Most Read:  1) Jeff Pu on ASIC Updates on TPU v9 ; 2) Michelle Jing 
on PCB Spec in 2H27; 3) Alicia Xia on CPO Acceleration; 4) Jeff Pu on NAND Re-
acceleration; 5) Jeff Pu on TSMC 3Q26 Preview.

### Page 3

Sector Report | Technology

GFHK Tech Important Stock Calls  
In the past one month, our key reports and stock calls are: 
Jeff Pu on ASIC Updates on TPU v9, reported date on Oct 13: We expect near-term MTK headwinds 
from TPU v9 delays but remain constructive on MTK/MRVL longer term. Humufish’s redesign could 
push tape-out to Jan -27, MP tape -out to 2Q27 and shipments to 1Q28, while EMIB -T yields could 
recover from 45–50%. Rising TPU allocations to Anthropic could constrain Gemini compute, though 
higher internal allocation in 2H26 may support future models. SemiAnalysis expects Anthropic/OpenAI 
to each add 5 –10GW in 2027 vs. a few GW for Google. For TPU v10 (4Q28 /1Q29), MTK/GUC’s 
Icefish remains the only confirmed mainstream SKU, while Broadcom-led Merope faces AMD/Marvell 
competition, with both potentially testing EMIB-T. 
Michelle Jing on PCB Spec in 2H27, reported date on Oct 5: Some market watchers expect Nvidia 
to shift from PTFE to hydrocarbon resin (HC) for the VR300 NVL576 switch tray. HC—one of the glass 
free alternatives within PTFE hybrid architectures—has only recently passed SI testing and has yet to 
enter PCB MP, leaving it roughly three months behind PTFE in qualification. On the contrary, we 
believe that PTFE offers superior performance for high frequency, high speed designs. With VR300 
NVL576 shipments expected in 2H27 and the final configuration likely set by end 26 —and with a 
leading Chinese PCB ma ker already achieving meaningful PTFE hybrid yields —we maintain that 
PTFE hybrid remains the most probable solution for next year’s switch board. 
Alicia Xia on CPO Acceleration , reported date on  Sept 29 : Nvidia’s CPO scale -out switch is 
progressing faster than expected, with FAU yields improving. We forecast CPO switch shipments at 
15k/16k/30k in 4Q26/1Q27/2Q27 and 100k in 2027. For Nvidia/AWS scale -up, CPO and NPO 
continue in parallel, with full NPO adoption expected at NVL576 launch. Feynman is also devel oping 
both variants, with CPO potentially using QM6 + six 6.4T OEs. We shift AWS Trainium 4 launch to 
1Q28 and forecast global scale-up OE demand at 6.5m/42m in 2027E/28E. 
Jeff Pu on NAND Re -acceleration, reported date on Sept 20 : According to TrendForce, NAND 
demand is stronger than expected as U.S. CSPs scale AI infrastructure, with eSSD demand nearly 
2x prior forecasts. We raise NAND contract price growth to +21%/+17%/+15% QoQ in 
3Q26/4Q26/1Q27, while hyperscaler inventories decline. Supply remains disci plined at 15%/21%/20% 
YoY bit growth in 2025 –27. Demand is supported by agentic AI, vector databases and KV -cache 
offloading, where eSSD offers lower cost/bit than DRAM and better power efficiency than HDD. 
Jeff Pu on TSMC 3Q26 Preview , reported date on  Oct 7 : TSMC shares hit record highs on 
expectations ahead of the print and potential Terafab collaboration. We expect largely inline 3Q26 
results, capex and full-year guidance, with softer 4Q26 margin (67.2%). Management should reaffirm 
2026 capex at the ceiling and mid-40s YoY growth, while we forecast 37% growth in 2027 on sustained 
AI/CPU/networking demand and price hikes. Key focuses include margins, long -term capex/U.S. 
expansion, large-reticle packaging and A14. We raise 2026E/27E EPS by 2%/8% on hig her N2/N3 
capacity and operating leverage, and roll to 20x 2027E P/E, lifting TP to NT$2,960.

### Page 5

Sector Report | Technology

September Monthly Data  
TSMC September sales of NT$511.9bn, -0.6% MoM and +54.6% YoY, with total 3Q revenue reaching 
NT$1.49T, +17.6 QoQ, slightly ahead of expectations of +14%. The solid performance was well 
supported by ASIC, A20 Pro, and Rubin. For 3Q26, we expect gross margin to be 67.8%, higher than 
guidance of 65-67% and consensus of 66.4% . For 4Q26, we expect another 12% QoQ growth with 
GM at 67.2%, both ahead of street’s estimates of +10% QoQ and 66% margin. Ahead of the earnings 
call, key focus include gross margin trajectory, long -term capex discipline, and the execution of its 
U.S. fab expansion. 
Rubin to accelerate:  Hon Hai revenue last month was NT$1.16T, +26 % MoM, +38% YoY, mainly 
driven by iPhone build.  Quanta and Wistron delivered -23% and -17% MoM respectively, due to AI 
racks’ shipping timing differences. Of note, Pegatron surprised the market with +143.2% MoM revenue 
surge fueled by initial GB300 rack shipments to neocloud customers including Lambda and Together 
AI. This reaffirms our thesis of robust NVL72 order momentum across Neoclouds. Looking ahead, 
VR200 is ramping now, with volume deployment accelerating into 2027. We maintain our forecast of 
75k and 92k NVL72 (GB+VR) rack shipments for 2026 and 2027, respectively. Concurrently, Wiwynn 
delivered another solid month with revenue expanding 20.0% MoM, underpinne d by resilient GP 
server demand alongside the ramp of Trainium 3. 
ASIC ramping up: On design service vendors fronts, driven  by T3 ramp, Alchip’s Sep revenue 
reached a record NT$9.9 8bn. Backed by secured incremental 3nm wafer allocation and improving 
yields, management raised its 2027 revenue guidance, now targeting growth above the previous 50% 
YoY baseline. That said, we push out T4 shipment timeline into 1Q28. GUC delivered a strong beat 
with monthly revenue reaching a record NT$7.145bn (+22.4% MoM, +97.8 % YoY), driven by robust 
Axion 2 CPU demand. Beyond its engagement with top 4 CSPs, multi -year order visibility into 2027 
and beyond is further bolstered by high-margin (likely TK1) automotive ASIC designs. 
PC forecast trimmed: NB shipments from top 5 ODMs were 9.7m in Sep, with 3Q26 shipments down 
17% QoQ and 26% YoY. Global PC OEM shipments contracted 20.1% YoY in 3Q26, worse than 
expected, post robust 1H26 pull -ins with high pricing points and weak macro. For 2027, we project 
the industry will decline a further 5% YoY. On the competitive front, tier -1 vendors such as Lenovo, 
Dell, and HP are not immune to this downturn, while entry-level laptops (low cost models from China) 
continue to see stronger relative demand.  
Substrate/CCL Upcycle Intact: Unimicron continued to deliver another all-time high sales with +5% 
MoM and 65% YoY. Supported by ABF upward pricing of ~15% in 2H26 (likely more next year) and 
higher mix of AI products, we expect a meaningful GM uptick for this quarter with momentum to sustain 
into 2027 . In the meantime, ZengDing’s revenue rose 28% MoM and 36% YoY mainly driven by  
iPhone, 1.6T mSAP, and Rubin’s HDI. On the CCL front, bolstered by ongoing price hikes and strong 
demand from GP servers, ITEC revenue grew 15.5% MoM and 111% YoY, and NanYa Plastics 
recorded +12.5% MoM and +55% YoY. Meanwhile, EMC and TUC both posted another single digit 
growth MoM on sales.

Downstream tech – Smartphone & Apple  
We maintain Global smartphone forecast of -14% in 2026  
According to IDC, the preliminary 2Q26 smartphone data points to a still ‑soft market with uneven 
regional dynamics, and their commentary continues to emphasize structural rather than cyclical 
headwinds. Smartphone shipments remain under pressure from weak replacement cycles, cautious 
consumer spending, and limited breakthrough features. IDC typically highlights that “growth is 
concentrated in the premium tier,” driven by ecosystem lock ‑in, while the mass market remains 
challenged by price sensitivity. They also tend to note that China and parts of emerging markets show 
only modest stabilization, not a full recovery, and that vendors are prioritizing margin discipline over 
volume.  
Overall, by factoring in higher iPhone 17/17 Pro volume, we fine tune our global smartphone shipment 
forecast to -14% YoY from -15% YoY. 
For 2027, we expect global smartphone shipments to be flattish YoY. That said, 2026 -2027 will be 
one of the most challenging periods for the industry, driven primarily by sharply higher memory prices, 
which have already pushed OEMs to reduce shipments and raise device prices.

iPhone 18 Pro/18 PM demand lukewarm  
iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max wait-time lukewarm: iPhone 18 Pro and iPhone 18 Pro Max pre-orders began 
on Sept 12 in the U.S. and many other countries. Generally speaking, the wait-time appears lukewarm, 
with: 1) 3–4 weeks for 256GB/512GB Pro Max across all colors, as well as Burgundy 1TB in the U.S.；
2) 2–3 weeks for Glacier 256GB/512GB Pro Max in China; 3) 1–2 weeks for Black/Glacier 1TB Pro 
Max in the U.S. ；4) immediate availability for Silver 1TB and mos t 2TB Pro Max models in the U.S, 
as well as most iPhone 18 Pro configurations except for Burgundy 256GB in the U.S. We attribute the 
lukewarm wait-time to limited spec upgrade, higher pricing (especially for large storage models), etc. 
We also expect the Pro Max to be impacted by iPhone Duo and the criticism of heavy weight.  
iPhone Duo feedback generally positive 
The iPhone Duo, priced at $1,999 in the U.S., opened pre‑orders on Oct 16 and officially launched on 
Oct 23. Early reviews were positive, highlig hting its polished folding experience, high ‑quality inner 
display, and improved multitasking in iOS 27, but also point to trade ‑offs including a heavier and 
thicker form factor, a visible crease, and a dual ‑camera. Due to hinge constraints, we are reducing  
the iPhone Duo EMS build plan from 7m to 6m units in 2026. We also lower the iPhone 18 Pro/Pro 
Max build plan to 72m units given the constraints associated with the adjustable‑aperture camera.

Sector Report | Technology

Downstream tech – PC 
Global PC units down to -12% in 2026, followed by another decline in 2027 
On Oct 8, IDC reported 3Q26 global PC shipments fell 20.1% YoY, missing street expectations of a 
10–15% decline. The sharper contraction was primarily driven by front -loaded demand in 1H26, 
elevated ASPs, and broader macro weakness. This aligns with our prior view that ODM build 
momentum moderated starting in end -2Q26, amid the rising concerns over channel inventory. 
Meanwhile, the top 5 ODMs also saw 3Q26 shipments decline 26% YoY. Looking ahead, we model 
4Q26 shipments to continue 20% YoY drop (flattish QoQ), underperforming normal seasonality. 
On the component front, we expect memory de -spec to normalize. End -users still require sufficient 
DRAM capacity (16GB+) to handle on -device AI workloads, and PC DDR 5 supply constraints have 
eased compared to Server’s. On pricing, new models will still have higher pricing points to pass 
through 2H component cost increases across memory, CPU, etc. That said, we anticipate price hikes 
will remain measured vs 1H26 given sluggish end-market demand. 
Overall, reflecting weaker than expected 3Q26 results post pull -ins and a muted 4Q outlook, we trim 
our 2026 PC shipment forecast to -12% YoY. For 2027, we project shipments to decline further by 5% 
YoY, as higher ASP and persistent macro headwinds continue to weigh on demand.

manufactured in China continue to gain traction across China and Southeast Asia. Looking ahead, we 
expect value-tier models to remain the primary driver in sustaining ongoing PC shipments.

Upstream tech – AI Semi & Supply Chain  
U.S. CSP capex to be +85%/+53%/+20% in 2026/2027/2028 
Given capex expands further into 2027, CSPs free cash flow are undergoing significant pressure and 
turning negative except Microsoft, such that shifting to debt financing for their massive capex. In 2025, 
Top 5 CSPs’ total debt issuance reached $108bn, or 24% of total capex . Per our Addressing 2028 
Capex and AI Platform Value Shift report on Aug 6, we forecast Top 5 CSPs’ debt issuance to reach 
$257bn/$419bn in 2026/2027, or 31%/35% of total capex.  Assuming debt issuance to grow in 
2028/2029 at a mild pace, we believe U.S. CSPs' balance sheet (except Oracle) to remain very healthy, 
due to net leverage (EBITDA basis) of only 0.5 and the net debt-to-equity ratio of only 18% by 2029.  
Despite healthy balance sheet for most CSPs, we expect most to safeguard balance sheets beyond 
2027 amid widening spreads and FCF profiles, with the focus shifting to returns. Together with a swing 
factor of competition from Chinese LLMs, we expect U.S. CSPs to decelerate capex expansion. We 
forecast Top 5 CSPs capex of $1,512bn/1,739bn in 2028/2029, or +20%/15% YoY, down from +53% 
in 2027.

Server CPU TAM to grow at 49% CAGR by 2030 
As the orchestration core of AI infrastructure buildouts, the Server CPU has gained significant traction 
over the past few months. This is driven by the Agentic AI/inference trend, fueled by the breakthrough 
of OpenClaw and Anthropic’s ARR surging to $44bn  in April. For instance, in latest earnings calls, 
AMD projected a >35% CAGR for Server CPU TAM by 2030, and Intel said the shift toward agentic 
AI is driving a structural demand for CPU, where the GPU-to-CPU ratio is tightening from 8:1 in training 
to 4:1 in inference. Based on our estimates, assuming inference accounts for 90% of AI workloads 
and GPU-to-CPU ratio for AI servers to reach 1:1.2 in 2030, we now expect Server CPU TAM to grow 
96%/71% in 2026/2027, reaching $215bn by 2030 from $30bn in 2025, with a 5-yr CAGR of 49%.

CoWoS tracker: fine tuning upwardly for total capacity 
Following our earlier raise in May Edition on May 12 in which we revised up TSMC’s CoWoS capacity 
from 175KPM to 195KPM by end-2027, the Street has moved up to our numbers , and we have fine 
tune our estimate to 200KPM by end-2027 on our Sept Edition on Sept 14.

2026/2027/2028, and ASE’s CoWoS capacity of 15KPM/45KPM/55KPM by end 2026/2027/2028.  
For fabless, we keep our CoWoS estimates largely unchanged for AI GPU/ASIC. For our accelerator 
model, we maintain +98% YoY 2027E, and for 2028E, we now expect it to reach the TAM of $1,000bn.  
In our ASIC Update report on Oct 13, we lowered our TPU units in 2028 to 10m, vs. 8.9m in 2027.

Sector Report | Technology

Upstream tech – Optical 
Strong demand for pluggable through 2028. 
We expect that the total demand for 800G/1.6T will reach 90mn/80mn in 2027, increased by 
80%/167%+, mainly driven by: 1) Nvidia, Google and AWS and other ASIC’s Accelerators’ demand 
are ramping up; 2) GPU/ASIC scale -out bandwidth continuously  increase drives increasing 
GPU/ASIC-to-optical module ratio; 3) We expect that TPU for 2027 will be 8.9m and Google fully 
transitions to large -scale clusters in 2026 and the scale -up layer will adopt optical interconnects. In 
addition, as we expect that CPO/NPO will start since 2H27, represents a pure incremental opportunity 
for optical interconnects supply chain, benefiting FAU, laser, shuffle box, connector and OE players, 
including LITE, GLW, TSEM, Sumitomo and Browave. We also like Marvell and SMTC, for the 
TIA/Driver used in NPO OE.  
Scale-out bandwidth increase driving optical-module deployment 
For NVIDIA, each Rubin GPU is equipped with two CX9 NIC chips, doubling the scale-out bandwidth 
compared with Blackwell. We further estimate that Rubin Ultra will adopt four CX9 chips per GPU, 
leading to another 2 times increase in scale-out bandwidth, continuing NVIDIA’s consistent bandwidth-
doubling trajectory. Accordingly, we expect the ratio of 1.6T optical modules has risen from 1:2.5 to 
1:5 in Rubin. From CoWoS perspective, we expect 5m/2m Blackwell/Rubin and 20m 1.6T in 2026. 
For Google, we believe that Goo gle fully transition to large -scale clusters in 2026 and the scale -up 
layer will adopt optical interconnects. We believe that in the scale-up domain, the ratio of TPU to 1.6T 
optical modules is approximately 1:1.5; In the scale-out domain, the ratio is around 1:2.5, suggesting 
the overall TPU-to-optics ratio is approximately 1:4 (1.6T equivalent).

Nvidia/CSPs’ Scale-out/up CPO/NPO  
Within NVIDIA’s next -gen NVL576 and broader CSPs’ AI infrastructure plans, CPO and NPO are 
developed side -by-side to expand GP U/ASIC interconnect bandwidth: CPO is the ideal long -term 
design for lower power and latency yet faces headwinds from complicated advanced packaging, and 
yield risks, while NPO could deliver most short -reach optical performance gains with far better 
manufacturability and deployment flexibility, making NPO a reliable near -term scaling path and CPO 
the ultimate end -state architecture. NPO partially removes module -side DSP and shifts signal 
conditioning and equalization to system designs, greatly lifting the value of TIAs and laser drivers that 
take over part of traditional DSP functions; we estimate each 3.2T optical engine contains tens of 
dollars worth of combined TIA and driver chips, a clear improvement over standard pluggable optics. 
Our shipment forecasts at 6.5m/42.4m NPO optical engines in CY27/28.  
Scale-up domain to increase by NVL576 to compensate lower memory: With lower memory spec, we 
expect Nvidia to increase the scale -up domain by optics to enhance performance. According to 
Nvidia’s GTC 2026, th e optical scale -up fabric allows Nvidia to expand Rubin Ultra to an NVL576 
scale-up. Regarding CPO and NPO which are being parallelly developed, we expect NPO to be a 
high reliability scaling path for NPO. We now expect its scale -up to adopt NPO in Rubin U ltra and 
Feynman platform and estimate OE shipments of Nvidia’s platform to be 6.0m/19.7m in 2027/2028.

### Page 25

Sector Report | Technology

Nvidia’s NPO OE to be 6.0m/19.7m in 2027/2028 
For Nvidia, we now expect its scale up to adopt NPO in Rubin Ultra and Feynman platform and 
estimate OE  shipments of Nvidia’s platform to be 6.0m/19.7m in 2027/2028, based on full NPO 
adoption at NVL576 launch given NPO maturity. Coupled with other CSP’s platform, we estimate total 
OE market to be 6.5m/42.4m in 2027/2028.  
TIA/Driver to benefit from NPO 
NPO partially removes module -side DSP and shifts signal conditioning and equalization to system 
designs, greatly lifting the value of TIAs and laser drivers that take over part of traditional DSP 
functions; we estimate each 3.2T optical engine contains tens o f dollars worth of combined TIA and 
driver chips, a clear improvement over standard pluggable optics.  
A key architectural shift under NPO is the partial removal of DSP functionality at the module level, with 
signal conditioning and equalization increasing ly redistributed into system -level design. This 
significantly elevates the importance of TIA (transimpedance amplifier) and laser driver components, 
which effectively replace a portion of traditional DSP value. We estimate that each 3.2T optical engine 
will embed high tens of dollars of combined TIA and driver content, representing a meaningful step -
up versus traditional pluggable optics architectures.  
From a competitive standpoint, we expect Marvell to remain the primary beneficiary of both NPO OE 
given i ts stronger positioning high speed TIA/Driver. Semtech is well positioned as a secondary 
beneficiary through its exposure to high-speed analog signal chain components, particularly TIAs and 
laser drivers, which scale directly with optical engine proliferat ion under NPO. We forecast 
6.5mn/42.4mn NPO OE shipments in CY27/28. Based on our estimated front-end content per OE and 
a 30% market share assumption for Semtech, we estimate its NPO revenue of $315/936mn. 
Stock Implication 
Generally speaking, scale-up CPO/NPO is an incremental opportunity for optical interconnect supply 
chain, as it aims to replace copper and has no impact on scale -out side. Additionally, scale up and 
scale out CPO solution share the similar suppliers. That said, we expect the key benefic iaries to be 
components such as FAU, CW laser, ELSFP, shuffle, PIC, TIA and Driver. We: 1) prefer Lumentum/ 
COHR due to the demand upside for CW laser, and also like local cw laser players; 2) expect Browave 
(3163 TT) to benefit, given the sizable contribu tion of CPO’s shuffle box considering ~55% market 
share, $5-6k ASP and a volume ramp in 2H26; 3) we also like TSEM, given CSP preferring NPO 
solution for their ASICs scale up interconnection. 4) expect GLW to benefit, given the second supplier 
of shuffle b ox and main supplier of MMC connector/fiber. 5) Marvell/Semtech will also benefit from 
CSP/Nvidia’s NPO solution, since TIA and driver will upgrade in the NPO Optical Engine.

### Page 28

Sector Report | Technology

Google’s 6D Torus & Beneficiaries.  
We expect Google will migrate its TPU interconnect from 3D to 6D torus, re -organizing the 64 -chip 
elemental block from 4× 4× 4 to 2× 2× 2× 2× 2× 2. External optical links per block rise from 96 to 384, with 
optical ports per chip scaling increase from 1:1.5 to 1:6, driving 4x coherent lite transceivers and OCS 
demand. The trajectory aligns with NVIDIA's CPO/NPO move. Both are interconnect scaling that 
expands direct chip-to-chip bandwidth and reduces hop count, with TPU via a 6D torus and NVIDIA 
via CPO/NPO for Scale-up. 
Google’s migration from 3D  to 6D Torus: We expect Google to migrate its TPU interconnect from a 
3D to a 6D torus topology, re-organizing the elemental 64-chip building block from a 4× 4× 4 3D Torus 
cube into a 2× 2× 2× 2× 2× 2 hyper-cube. In the current 3D design, each chip connects to 6 nearest 
neighbors across X/Y/Z axes and the cube exposes 16 optical ICI links on each of its 6 faces, totaling 
96 external optical links per cube (~1.5 optical ports per chip). Under the 6D design, each dimension 
has 64 external optical links (2⁶ chips, on e outward link per chip per dimension), for a total of 6 ×  64 
= 384 external optical links (~6 optical ports per chip). The per-chip optical port count thus scales from 
1.5 to 6. Combined with Scale -out at an estimated 1:2.5, this brings the aggregate TPU -to-port ratio 
to 1:8.5. 
Lower latency and closer to non -blocking Scale-up: For a 4,096 -chip superpod, a 3D torus (4× 4× 4) 
carries a worst-case diameter of 24 hops, whereas a 6D torus (2× 2× 2× 2× 2× 2) halves that to 12 hops 
(2× 6), directly compressing collective-operation and latency. Higher dimensionality also spreads traffic 
across 6 orthogonal paths, improving bisection bandwidth and moving closer to non-blocking chip-to-
chip communication. Critically, shortening each edge from 4 to 2 nodes eliminates deep mu lti-hop 
electrical paths inside the block: the architecture deliberately reduces intra -cube copper 
communication and pushes a far larger share of traffic onto optical fiber. 
Key beneficiaries: The exact TPU generation for the adoption of 6D torus and initi al penetration rate 
remain unclear. That said, whether NVIDIA moves toward CPO/NPO or Google expands from 3D to 
6D, both are Scale -up strategies aimed at enabling greater inter -chip communication. Key 
beneficiaries include the 2.4T Coherent Lite module and the OCS supply chain, such as MEMS-based 
optical switch vendors, PIC/laser/DSP suppliers, and fiber/cabling vendors supporting the larger 
reconfigurable lightwave fabric.

Risks 
1) Alternative technologies; 2) AI demand and capex slowdown; 3) IT demand slowdown; 4) product 
delays.

## Document 388: MSCI、トルコ株式指数の変更を凍結し、格下げの可能性を警告 ～トルコ株式市場の混乱は長期化の様相、MSCIは改革の進捗を注視～

Source PDF: all report pdf/MSCI、トルコ株式指数の変更を凍結し、格下げの可能性を警告 ～トルコ株式市場の混乱は長期化の様相、MSCIは改革の進捗を注視～.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年10 月 16 日

1/3 
MSCI、トルコ株式指数の変更を凍結し、格下げの可能性を警告 
～トルコ株式市場の混乱は長期化の様相、MSCI は改革の進捗を注視～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ トルコ株式市場では、 投資信託による株式売却圧力を背景に下落基調が続いている。MSCI は
６月、一部投資信託による低流動性銘柄への保有集中や浮動株比率の歪みに懸念を示し、当
局に情報開示や市場監視の強化を求めた。背景には、政府が国内資金をリラ建て資産に誘導
したことで、低浮動株企業の上場や投資信託への資金流入が拡大し、低流動性銘柄の株価上
昇と資金流入が相互に増幅する構造が形成されたことがある。 
○ その後、資本市場委員会（ＳＰＫ）が投資信託の保有上限を厳格化し、年末までに超過分の
解消を求めたことで資金循環が逆回転した。株価下落に伴う解約増加から、一部運用会社が
解約請求に応じられない事態となり、現金確保を目的に流動性の高い銘柄にも売りが波及し
た。 当局は資金供給の拡充や投資信託の取引停止・清算のほか、 関係者を刑事告発するなど、
早期の事態収拾に向けた取り組みを強化している。 
○ MSCI は10 月15 日、 トルコ株式指数の変更を凍結し、2027 年６月までに十分な市場改革が進
まなければ、新興国市場からフロンティア市場などへの格下げを検討する可能性を示した。
格下げとなればパッシブ投資家を中心に資金流出が加速する懸念がある。加えて、利下げ観
測に伴うリラ安から家計資金が外貨や金に流れる可能性もあり、市場混乱の長期化が実体経
済や政府の政策運営に波及するリスクに注意が必要である。

トルコ株の下落基調が続いている（図１）（注１）。背景には、現金確保を目的に投資信託が株式
の売却圧力を強めていることがある。今年６月、指数算出会社である MSCI が一部の投資信託によ
る株式保有の集中や、流動性の低い銘柄に関する警告を発したことがきっかけとなった。 
具体的には、特定の中小上場企業と密接な関係にある投資信託の保有分が、浮動株比率の推計値
を人為的に押し上げていると、海外の機関投資家から懸念が寄せられていることを明らかにした。
こうした懸念は、当該銘柄の指数構成比率を歪めることにつながる。

注１ 10 月９日付レポート「トルコ金融市場の混乱が収まらない」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/666443.html）

### Page 2

2/3 
このため、同社は当局に対して、実質
的な所有者に関する詳細かつ適時の情
報開示のほか、 協調的な取引行動に対す
る強固な監視と取り締まり、 構造的に歪
められた浮動株を特定するための透明
性が高いルールに基づく枠組みの構築
を求める考えを示した。そのうえで、11
月に実施する指数見直しまでに十分か
つ具体的な進展が見られない場合、 同国
と対象銘柄の適切な取り扱いについて
協議を開始する可能性があるとした。 
こうした懸念が高まった背景には、2023 年に財務相に返り咲いたシムシェキ氏の下、 同国政府が
国内貯蓄をリラ建て資産にとどめる取り組みを進めたことがある。そのため、2021 年に導入された
為替保護型預金（ＫＫＭ：リラ安による為替下落率が預金金利を上回った場合、その差額を政府・
中銀が補填する制度）を廃止し、通常のリラ建て預金への移行を促進したほか、株式や投資信託な
どにも資金を誘導した。 
一方で、同国では高金利状態が長期化しており、政府は企業に借入ではなく、株式市場の活用に
よる資金調達の積極化を推進したため、浮動株比率が低い企業が数多く上場した。こうした銘柄は
市場で売買可能な株式数が限られ、株価が大きく変動しやすいことで、価格形成に歪みが生じた。
さらに、 その後の投資ブームを追い風に投資信託が急拡大し、 投資家にとっては株式投資の間口が
広がる一方、リスクの集中が懸念された。具体的には、投資信託に組み入れられる比較的流動性が
低い銘柄への資金流入が活発化し、 株価上昇に伴い運用収益が向上した結果、 新たな資金流入を生
む循環が形成された。 
規制機関である資本市場委員会（ＳＰＫ）は８月、投資信託の純資産に対する組入比率、発行済
株式数に対する保有割合など保有ポジションの上限を厳格化し、2026 年末までに超過分の解消を
義務付ける規制変更を発表した。具体的には、10 月末までに超過分の３分の１、11 月末までに３
分の２、12 月末までに完全解消を求めている。 
その結果、 株価を押し上げてきた資金流入の循環が逆回転し、 株価下落により投資信託の解約請
求が膨らんだ。 さらに、 複数の資産運用会社が投資家による解約請求に応じられないと発表する事
態に発展した。 流動性の低い保有資産の売却が困難になり、 現金確保を目的に流動性の高い銘柄に
売却圧力が強まったことで、市場全体に売り圧力が広がっている。 
市場の混乱を受けて、 中銀は流動性確保を目的とするレポ資金の供給額引き上げのほか、 銀行間
資金市場における銀行の借入限度額も大幅に引き上げた。ＳＰＫも、複数の投資信託の取引停止・
清算を命じたほか、関係者を刑事告発するといった動きをみせている。 
こうしたなか、MSCI は 10 月 15 日、トルコ株式指数に関する変更を凍結することを明らかにし
た。当局による市場改革を評価するためにさらなる時間が必要とし、2027 年６月までに十分な進展

## Document 396: Open mouth operations (2026-10-28)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Open mouth operations (2026-10-28).pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 12
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#australia #luciellis #rba
Open mouth operations
By Luci Ellis
Chief Economist Westpac Group, Westpac
13:34 October 23 2026 Share
The RBA does not want to promise what it will do, but it is increasingly trying to tell us what
it thinks.
Central banks’ communication is carefully scrutinised for every nuance. Lately, there seems to have
been more RBA communication to pore over. The method of delivery has also evolved. This has

### Page 2

Market participants pore over every utterance by central banks for clues to future rate moves. They will
have plenty to pore over in the run-up to the November RBA decision. After the flurry of fireside chats
this week, ’60 Minutes’ will run a pre-recorded interview with chief economist Sarah Hunter on Sunday.
Next Wednesday, the whole senior policy team will appear before Senate Estimates, just as the
quarterly CPI is released. (This timing will have been the Senate’s choice, not the RBA’s.) After the

decision will come the Statement on Monetary Policy, a media release and press conference. More
speeches will follow, including one by the Deputy Governor on the Thursday.
RBA communication does seem to have changed, not just following the RBA Review but also more
recently. There may be fewer policy meetings each year, but the communication outside that certainly
feels more frequent than a couple of years ago. Back in 2024, speaking engagements felt a little

sparse outside the post-meeting media conference and parliamentary appearances. We have counted
58 public engagements so far this year on the Speeches page on the RBA’s website. This compares
with 58 for the whole of last year and 52 in 2024. Excluding the ‘compulsory’ press conferences and
parliamentary appearances, fewer than half of the 2024 events were related to economics, markets
and monetary policy.
The ‘how’ has changed
The way the RBA communicates with markets has changed as well. There are fewer set-piece

speeches on policy and a lot more semi-scripted ‘fireside chats’. Compared with a few years ago, there
are more interviews across a wide range of media. This is a big change from the pre-Review world. It
was a huge deal when the previous Governor appeared on the 7:30 Report; it is hard to imagine him
agreeing to an Assistant Governor appearing on 60 Minutes.
Part of the change reflects increased efforts to reach a general audience. Central banks want to explain

why they do what they do. This matters especially when interest rates are rising but the household
sector is not the source of the inflation.
Speaking differently to different audiences can sometimes confuse markets. As the RBA’s Chief
Communication Officer highlighted in a recent panel discussion, the post-meeting media conference is
designed to speak to the public. Market participants and economists certainly tune in, but we are not
sometimes caused confusion when tone seems to vary. Different audiences call for different tone and

varying complexity.
The content has evolved, too. RBA officials are increasingly using speeches directed to professional
audiences to discuss more technical estimates of inferred variables such as neutral rates and
financial conditions. They are also highlighting that monetary policy combats inflation in more ways
than just squeezing mortgage holders.
One thing has not changed: the RBA is not in the business of providing ‘forward guidance’ promises

about the future path of interest rates. It does, however, give more running commentary about how it
is interpreting recent data. These interpretations are themselves useful signals about what the RBA
thinks it might do.

### Page 3

the primary audience. So if you think that what the Governor said in the media conference sounded
more ‘dovish’ than what another official says in a more market-oriented forum, it is partly because she
is trying to convey that the RBA understands that its recent policy actions are making things harder for
many Australians. But this is in much the same way that a doctor might say, “This might hurt a bit.” It
does, but that will not stop the treatment.

A related issue is that it is easier to control tone in pre-drafted material such as the minutes and set-
piece speeches than in unstructured Q&A. Senior central bankers are trained to be careful with their
words in unscripted settings. If the tone differs, the minutes are still more likely to say what the Board
really means than an off-the-cuff answer.
Greater communication with the broader public might have reduced the pressure to make the set-piece

speeches more accessible. Research on central bank communication often uses measures such as
the Flesch–Kincaid Grade Level and Flesch Reading Ease score. A comprehensive analysis was
beyond the scope of this note. However, a spot comparison suggests four speeches from 2026 (1, 2, 3,
4) were harder to read than four from 2021 (1, 2, 3, 4). The 2021 speeches scored in the 11 to 12
range, which is high-school-level complexity. The 2026 speeches scored between 12 and 15, which is

academic or technically dense text requiring university-level education. (Including two of my own 2021
speeches is an obvious caveat, but the two speeches by then-Governor Phil Lowe also scored better
on these metrics than the sample from this year. It should be noted, though, that readability metrics do
not necessarily imply better clarity if the concepts discussed are more abstract.)
The ‘what’ has changed
As noted above, the content of RBA communication has evolved, too. As its intellectual framework has

begun to put more weight on the balance of supply and demand, and those invisible ‘star’ variables
(NAIRUs, neutral real rates, ‘financial conditions’, etc.), the communication to professional audiences
has increasingly focused on those topics.
More recently, the RBA has also placed more emphasis on the full range of transmission channels from
interest rates to inflation (though not, as we have noted previously, the labour supply channel). This is

understandable in a world of supply shocks. People need to understand that monetary policy is not
simply an exercise in hitting mortgage borrowers. We doubt they would be comforted, though, by the
Deputy Governor’s explanation this week that the ‘cashflow channel’ was the smallest of the
transmission channels from interest rates to inflation, with the exchange rate much more prominent.
That implies a lot of pain for a small group, for little effect on inflation. It raises the question of whether

other policy tools or institutional arrangements might get the same effect on inflation through the other
channels without hitting a minority of households disproportionately.
We have also seen a move away from using catchphrases to summarise the position. From ‘narrow
path’ to ‘not ruling anything in or out’ to ‘cautious and predictable’, once entrenched, a phrase can
outlast its usefulness.

### Page 4

Related articles
One thing that has not changed is the way the minutes record the presentations to the Board and the
members’ discussion. The minutes rarely convey much new information beyond the post-meeting
statements. Sometimes, though, the minutes reveal the Board’s reaction to the staff’s analysis. The
August 2024 minutes were a good example: the discussion highlighted just how sensitive the forecasts
were to key judgements by the staff.
The ‘why’ has not changed

Despite these changes, the underlying objectives of the RBA’s communication have not changed. It
wants to explain – and perhaps justify – its decisions. And it wants to describe how it interprets
incoming data, especially inflation, the labour market and the balance of supply and demand.
The RBA, like any central bank, is not really in the business of providing ‘forward guidance’ about
future moves. Circumstances change, so it can never guarantee a particular future path for interest

rates. Some academic literature argues that promising to keep rates low for a long time (or high, for
that matter) independently amplifies the transmission of monetary policy beyond the effect of current-
period interest rates alone, by shifting the short end of the yield curve. Central banks including the RBA
have certainly tried jawboning in this way from time to time. The prevailing view, though, is that the
potential cost of reputational loss if things do not turn out as predicted outweighs the benefits of a

slightly stronger transmission mechanism.
In this context, it is the interpretation of data that provides the signal about future policy directions. This
is why it was noteworthy that the Governor still characterised the labour market as ‘a little tight’ after the
September meeting, even though the Board’s post-meeting communication did not. We have been
noting for some time that the RBA staff have a downbeat view of supply capacity. Together with the

RBA’s oft-stated concerns about inflation, this is why the Deputy Governor’s ‘hawkishness’ did not
surprise us.

## Document 456: US Equity Views_ AI capex, required revenues, and what's priced in the AI trade

Source PDF: all report pdf/US Equity Views_ AI capex, required revenues, and what's priced in the AI trade.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs Publishing
Date: 2026-09-24
Pages: 16
Extraction status: ok

### Page 2

An update on AI and US equities

Both stocks and investor views relating to the AI trade have been exceptionally 
volatile during the last few months. Most of the volatility has been concentrated in the 
AI infrastructure complex, but the latest earnings season also drove sharp moves in the 
hyperscalers and among software stocks. 
Below we address three of the most common AI-related questions we have received 
from clients in recent weeks: 
1. What is the outlook for AI capex? 
2. What revenues are required to generate a sufficient return on recent AI 
investments? 
3. What outlook for AI is the equity market pricing?

1. What is the outlook for AI capex? 
The largest US hyperscalers are on track to spend $800 billion in capex in 2026 and 
consensus estimates imply they will spend $1.1 trillion in 2027. Consensus 2027 
capex estimates have been revised higher by $175 billion since the start of the Q2 
earnings season. Commentary from the hyperscalers during earnings season described a 
supply-constrained environment and large revenue backlogs, and these factors are 
driving the companies to tap both debt and equity markets to support additional 
investment spending. Based on consensus estimates, capex in 2027 is on track to reach a

### Page 3

larger share of GDP than any technological investment cycle since the railroad build-out 
in the late 1800’s. 
Our baseline forecast is that hyperscaler capex spending will increase substantially 
in 2027 but that the rate of both capex growth and upside surprises to consensus 
estimates will diminish relative to recent quarters. GS equity analysts estimate that 
hyperscaler capex will register $1.2 trillion in 2027 and $1.4 trillion in 2028. Their 
estimates are $130 billion and $100 billion above the consensus estimates, respectively, 
but nonetheless imply a deceleration in capex growth from nearly 100% in 2026 to 54% 
in 2027 and 12% in 2028.

The need for external financing and potential supply bottlenecks will likely 
constrain the rate of capex growth next year. Hyperscaler capex spending now 
exceeds cash flow from operations, which means large further growth will increasingly 
need funding via debt and equity issuance. While hyperscalers have added leverage, 
their balance sheets do not appear to be a binding constraint and their interest coverage 
ratios remain extremely healthy. Our credit strategists instead point to concentration in 
the investment grade market as the more relevant bottleneck, which will require 
additional funding from private credit and equity issuance. Potential government 
restrictions on data center construction or an intentional slowdown in frontier model 
roll-out could also act as potential constraints on spending, as could shortages of labor, 
power, memory, or other inputs. Constraints on capex represent a risk to the near-term 
earnings of AI infrastructure beneficiaries, but could help ease investor concerns about 
overbuilding.

### Page 4

Going forward, token demand growth will need to outpace declining token prices to 
support continued growth in investment spending. Frontier models are currently a 
key source of demand for hyperscaler compute. However, the rise of competitive 
open-source models has contributed to a decline in average token prices. Measures of 
frontier token demand slowed in July, simultaneous with sell-offs of many AI 
infrastructure stocks, but accelerated again in August. 
For most AI infrastructure companies, decelerating AI investment spending 
suggests decelerating EPS growth. AI infrastructure companies, and semis in 
particular, have been the clearest beneficiaries of the capex surge. Continued upward 
revisions to consensus 2027 capex estimates should support continued positive 
revisions to consensus 2027 EPS estimates for many AI infrastructure stocks, but the 
magnitude of revisions will likely diminish relative to what occurred in H1 2026.

### Page 5

2. What revenues are required to generate a return on AI investments? 
Our equity analysts estimate that the hyperscalers would need to generate annual 
AI revenues of roughly $300 billion in the next few years to break even on their 
average annual capex investments in 2026 and 2027. These estimates reflect their 
expectations for average annual AI capex in 2026 and 2027 for the US hyperscalers, 
including AMZN, GOOGL, META, MSFT, ORCL, and SPCX. The breakeven revenues 
required for this capex represent the sum of annual depreciation and operating 
expenses. Exhibit 8 provides an estimated sensitivity of required AI revenues the 
hyperscalers must generate in different return on invested capital (“ROIC”) scenarios. 
These required revenue estimates are anchored on average annual capex in 2026 and 
2027 and would argue for even larger estimates based on cumulative capex.

The hyperscalers’ current estimated AI revenues remain below the amount that 
would allow them to break even on their capex, but those revenues are growing 
quickly and revenue backlogs are sizable. The hyperscalers have started to monetize 
their AI investments. Year/year cloud revenue growth for AMZN, GOOGL, MSFT, and 
ORCL has accelerated from 25% in 2024 to 48% in Q2 2026. In dollar terms, annualized 
cloud revenues in excess of their 2024 growth rate registered nearly $70 billion in Q2 
and have been roughly doubling during the past few quarters. The three major US public 
cloud companies (AMZN, GOOGL, MSFT) have also announced a combined revenue 
backlog of $1.7 trillion, which provides line of sight into these companies’ ability to meet 
required revenue targets.

### Page 7

The magnitude of capex in 2026 and 2027 points to even larger “required” annual 
revenues in the next few years for the “application layer” of companies buying 
much of that compute from the hyperscalers. These revenues reflect the additional 
costs and profit margins of application companies on top of the “required” hyperscaler 
revenues selling compute. EBIT margins for large-cap software companies have 
averaged 40% during the past five years. We utilize EBIT margins rather than gross 
margins to include the costs of both training and inference, and assume additional 
expenses alongside compute. The range of potential required revenues is wide 
depending on which companies constitute the application layer and how their costs are 
structured.  
Exhibit 11 provides a range of potential “required” revenues based on hyperscaler 
ROIC and application layer EBIT margins. For example, the application layer would 
have to generate over $1 trillion in annual revenues if those companies had a 30% EBIT 
margin and the hyperscalers generated a 10%-20% return on invested capital. 
The “required” application layer revenues that would justify current capex are large 
but potentially achievable. For example, estimates of global advertising and software 
spending each equal roughly $1.5 trillion in 2026. Our economists estimate that total 
annual AI US labor productivity gains that will potentially accrue to capital total roughly 
$1.8 trillion. However, not all of these gains will necessarily accrue to the infrastructure 
and application layer companies. In addition, another year of strong capex growth in 
2028 would raise the hurdle further. AI-related revenues and capex needs would be 
substantially larger accounting for physical AI or the potential emergence of AGI.

### Page 8

3. What outlook for AI is the equity market pricing? 
While market participants express skepticism about the durability of AI 
infrastructure stocks’ earnings growth, that view is at least partially priced. The 
median AI infrastructure stock has de-rated from 32x consensus forward earnings in 
April 2026 to 22x today. In addition to tracking standard P/E multiples, comparing 
recent changes in market cap to recent changes in earnings can signal how long the 
market expects current earnings strength to persist. Among various AI infrastructure 
baskets, that ratio remains most elevated among optical networking, electrical grid, and 
liquid cooling stocks while expectations appear most conservative among 
semiconductors and utilities. 
Semiconductor valuations appear to embed particularly skeptical views regarding 
the sustainability of current earnings strength. Valuations have declined sharply 
among Semis stocks, which is typical among cyclical companies with strong near-term 
growth but uncertain medium-term growth. We believe the investment opportunity is 
more compelling in fabless chip stocks than in memory stocks. Our equity analysts are 
Buy-rated on NVDA and AVGO but Neutral-rated on MU.

### Page 9

Memory stock prices imply earnings will decline in coming years, but the degree of 
pessimism does not appear particularly extreme relative to past cycles. Memory 
stocks currently trade at a 2-year forward P/E of 4x, half their 15-year average, but 
Memory firms currently enjoy gross margins of roughly 80%, more than double their 
historical average. To bring the current P/E of memory stocks to its 15-year average, 
earnings in coming years would need to decline by roughly 50%. While this decline is 
large in absolute terms, memory is a cyclical industry and has experienced larger EPS 
declines, including outright negative earnings, during previous cycles. This pricing will 
prove too pessimistic if 2028 forecasts are realized, but many investors have expressed 
skepticism about rotating back into higher beta pockets of the infrastructure trade as 
capex growth begins to normalize.

### Page 10

In contrast, the fabless chip stocks also trade at discounted valuations but have had 
less extreme margin expansion. Margins for the largest fabless chip stocks are only 
modestly above their historical average. Earnings in the next few years would need to 
decline by about 10% to bring the P/E back to its historical average. This is similar to the 
magnitude of historical realized cyclical earnings declines for the group.

The likelihood of decelerating capex growth should be a positive catalyst for the 
hyperscalers. We previously laid out three conditions for the hyperscalers to regain 
leadership. These include decelerating macroeconomic growth, demonstrated 
monetization of AI investment, and decelerating capex growth. Our economists believe 
US economic growth is no longer accelerating. The hyperscalers have already started to 
demonstrate signs of monetization, and we expect capex growth will slow in 2027. 
Uncertainty about the length, magnitude, and return on investment for this capex cycle 
will likely persist. However, the hyperscalers have de-rated to their lowest P/E multiples 
in over a decade, and the group now trades just four turns above the median S&P 500 
company, its smallest premium on record. While investors have recently been focused 
more on the hyperscalers’ negative free cash flows than on their earnings, visibility into 
improving FCF as revenues accelerate and capex decelerates should be supportive for 
the stocks.

### Page 11

We expect the progression of the AI trade towards application roll-out will remain a 
source of return dispersion. We believe the “winners” and “losers” from the application 
layer will take several quarters to play out, as AI applications get built and adoption 
broadens. 
The threat of disruption had weighed on the share prices of many Software stocks 
earlier this year, but the group has rebounded sharply in recent weeks. Reported 
Software fundamentals have shown little sign of AI disruption, with annual recurring 
revenue growth for the median stock in GS analyst coverage accelerating slightly in 
recent quarters. A group of Software stocks has rallied by 45% and is now within 8% of 
its high reached in late 2025. Software stocks focused on IT infrastructure and 
cybersecurity have posted the strongest returns, while enterprise management software 
remain below their previous highs.

### Page 12

Consumer-facing businesses with high switching costs have also recently emerged 
as a potential area of disruption. The increasing use of agents, including by new 
consumer-facing tools such as Muse, may disrupt the business models of some 
consumer firms. Our colleagues in Global Banking & Markets developed a basket of 
“consumer inertia” stocks (GSXUSWCH) where user stickiness is driven more by effort 
required to take action rather than product differentiation. The basket includes 
insurance, telecom, travel, subscriptions, marketplaces, and fintech. The basket has 
lagged a sector-neutral hedge by 5 pp since mid-September.

### Page 13

Enterprise AI adoption remains in early stages but spending has recently 
accelerated, suggesting the impact of AI on corporate earnings should grow 
increasingly visible in coming quarters. The Census Bureau survey suggests that 40% 
of large US firms are using AI in the production of goods and services. Our analysis of Q2 
earnings commentary suggests roughly 50% of S&P 500 companies are using AI in the 
context of productivity, but very few companies have quantified the productivity uplift 
today. However, the revenue run rates of model providers and alternative measures of 
company spending on AI have accelerated during the last several months alongside the 
increasing use of agentic harnesses. 
In contrast with past periods of market exuberance, the equity market today does 
not appear to be pricing expectations of widespread productivity gains. Baskets of 
AI productivity beneficiaries have traded roughly sideways on a relative basis YTD, 
matching the trajectory of near-term forward EPS estimates. Similarly, the measure of 
market-implied long-term growth expectations in Exhibit 22 is only slightly above the 
historical average (for details, see US Equity Views: The next phases of the AI trade).

《地緣風險衝擊中東信用基本面 ，但緩衝能力仍佳 》
中東戰事持續外擴 ，除了美伊相互襲擊使得荷姆茲
海峽情勢依然緊張外 ，葉門胡塞武裝也對沙烏地阿
拉伯密集發動攻勢 ，柴油出口因此受阻 ，能源價格
處高位不墜 ，布蘭特原油 10月以來便維持在 100美
元/桶之上。過往中東國家往往是高油價的受惠者，
但受到能源生產和運輸中斷衝擊 ，國際貨幣基金10
月下調2026年中東及中亞地區經濟成長率預估值 1
個百分點至衰退，沙烏地阿拉伯更預估將衰退 3.3%。

新興主要貨幣企業債指數 6.64% A~BB級企業相對穩健
新興投資級債指數 5.55% 亞洲企業債供給有限支撐買氣，中東良好債信吸引力仍佳
新興非投資級債指數 7.50% BB級具升等潛力，拉美非投資級債可望因油價上漲而受惠
新興當地貨幣債指數 6.80% 風險溢酬反應貨幣政策較差情境，升息預期淡化後殖利率下行
人民幣債指數 2.20% 景氣不均衡復甦，寬鬆貨幣政策為主要支撐力
亞洲當地貨幣債指數 4.60% 亞洲國家貨幣政策觀望，短線維持中性看法

重點摘要 沙烏地阿拉伯 (以下簡稱沙國 )雖極力避免直接捲入
戰爭，但仍遭受衝突外溢效應波及 。戰爭不僅導致
沙國大幅增加補貼 ，軍事和安全支出以及融資費用
上升均加大財政支出 ，因此，即便油價上漲抵消產
量下降的影響而使石油收入高於預期，沙國2Q26預
算赤字佔 GDP比重仍達 7.1%，遠高於年度目標的
3.3%。儘管近期公布的 2027年預算計畫 (PBS)目標
是將預算赤字佔 GDP比率從2026年的4.9%(沙國官
方最新預估 )縮小到2027年的3.6%，但若戰爭長時
間 延 續， 此 目 標 或 難 以 達 成， 目 前 估 計2027 年
17420億里亞爾的債務目標亦將輕易被超越 。不過
即使如此 ，沙國目前負債占 GDP比重僅約 35%，

2027年市場最悲觀預期也大約40%左右，仍是低於
全球新興國家均值約80%的水準。
中東另一主要發債國卡達對天然氣出口依賴度高 ，
天然氣出口受阻也使得國際機構普遍預估卡達 2026
年經濟衰退10%以上、幾乎是2010年以來最重，所
幸卡達2026年以前累積巨額經常帳盈餘及外匯儲備
作為緩衝，即使在今年3月外部環境面臨極大壓力下，
卡達仍有 199億里亞爾的經常帳盈餘 ，累積過去四
季經常帳占 GDP比重仍達 13.5%，現今外匯存底足
以彌補3月的貿易逆差，並可維持４年以上。這些數
據都顯示戰爭對中東國家的經濟及對外償債能力確
實已帶來短線壓力 ，但過去累積的龐大緩衝使其具
備良好因應能力 ，信用違約交換 (CDS)目前並未因
定價油氣供應長期中斷而大幅攀升 ，今年雖持續發
債但債務壓力並不高 ，IMF更預期2027年中東國家
經濟將成長6.3%。然中東國家對能源出口依賴度仍
高，能源出口是否長期受阻則是後續觀察重點。

重點摘要 《經濟強勁帶來的高利率無損新興債利差穩步收斂 》
財政收支並非驅動新興主權信用利差的唯一因素 ，
總體經濟環境 、市場流動性均牽動利差走勢 ，因此
雖然自7月以來沙烏地阿拉伯等中東國家信用利差又
再擴大，但也大概在3年均值附近並不算高。以歷史
經驗來看，BB級主權國家利差通常對財政惡化程度
反應較大 ，可能是因為該評等僅低於投資等級一級 ，
財政變化容易牽動投資人對升等至投資級的預期與
投資部位的調整 ，而在投資等級的主權國家中 ，則
以匈牙利和波蘭這些債務負擔較重的主權債利差較
易受財政問題影響。
油價和利率是近期2項重要觀察變數，也牽動利差走
勢，不過由於新興主權債指數中約5成的組成為大宗
商品出口國、3成為石油出口國，反而能受惠油價上

漲，油價上漲時與利差走勢敏感度也較油價下跌時
低；然而 ，更重要的是高油價對通膨和政策應對的
二階影響 ，將導致高利率長時間延續的可能性並最
終衝擊實體經濟 ，進一步限制石油出口國利差收窄 。
以長期趨勢來看 ，利率水準的絕對值高低不必然影
響新興債利差 ，利率走勢反而和信用利差呈現小幅
負相關，利率走升反映的若是強勁經濟成長而非能
源供給衝擊 ，此時往往能伴隨信用利差收窄 ，反而
需留意的是利率波動放大及利率的大幅重新定價 ，
這才是牽動利差波動加大的主因 。目前總體經濟環
境部分或可與2004~2007年相比擬，當時在強勁成
長的背景下利率長時間在4%~5.3%高位震盪，但新
興主要貨幣主權債利差仍可收斂至 134點(此波低點
189點)，若經濟成長能在現今高油價和高利率環境
下持續強勁，利差則可望保持穩定。
重點摘要
• BB級主權債及債務負擔沈重
的投資級主權債利差較容易
受財政問題干擾
• 新興主權債指數中約 5成為石
油出口國 ，降低油價上漲對
利差走勢的敏感性
3
即使油價上升，新興債利差與油價也少見正相關

資料來源：JP Morgan，2026/10/9，，中國信託商業銀行，2026/10/19

不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行並不負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資之風險，並就投
資結果自行負責。未經本公司許可，本資料及訊息不得逕行抄錄、翻印或另作派發。本行以上市場資訊與分析，不涉及特定投資標的之建議。
重點摘要 《景氣及企業獲利續成長 ，企業債信改善趨勢延續 》
三大信評機構持續認可新興市場四大區域企業信用
評等的改善，3Q26新興市場企業債淨升等規模大幅
提高至760億美元，其中獲上調規模達到910億美元、
創下2025年第四季以來的最高水平 ，也已是連續第
5個季度改善。
3Q26新興市場4大區域評等調升數量均高於下調 ，
其中以亞洲改善動能最為顯著 ，主要貢獻來自於亞
洲金融業 ，全年升評 /降評比率已來到 1.8倍，逆轉
2019年降評多於升評的劣勢；拉美企業仍是新興市
場中違約率最高的區域 ，降評債券數量與其他區域
比也確實較高 ，但降評企業家數已是過去 10年來最
低，加上阿根廷主權債信獲上調帶動企業債 (主要是
國有企業)信評調升，拉美航空業者如南美航空集團

(LATAM Airlines)在油價維持高位環境下仍於3Q26
獲信評機構調升至 BB+，在企業債信分化環境下仍
有亮點 ，因此第三季拉美企業淨評級上調規模達
370億美元，反而創下2025年第三季以來的最佳季
度表現 ，全年共有 234家拉美企業信評得調升 。新
歐中東地區債信今年算是少數債信惡化的區域 ，很
大部分是受到戰爭及高油價影響所致 ，不過奈及利
亞和南非等主權評級上調仍帶來正向效果 ，梯瓦製
藥(Teva)評等更是上調至投資等級，帶來了120億美
元的貢獻，部分抵消地緣政治風險帶來的影響。
強勁的獲利成長持續支持新興企業穩健基本面 ，有
限的違約率輔以韌性十足的總經環境 、以及活躍但
不過度集中的初級市場 ，均是支持債信改善的原因 ，
預期在企業獲利仍看好的情境下 ，第四季企業債信
仍將朝正向發展。
• 3Q26新興市場企業債淨升等
規模大幅提高至760億美元，
創2025年第四季以來的最高
水平
• 新興市場4個主要區域評等調
升數量均高於下調 ，亞洲改
善動能最為顯著

4

不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行並不負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資之風險，並就投
資結果自行負責。未經本公司許可，本資料及訊息不得逕行抄錄、翻印或另作派發。本行以上市場資訊與分析，不涉及特定投資標的之建議。
重點摘要 《聯準會升息年 ，新興債雖有波動但正報酬機率高 》
觀察過往聯準會升息年度 ，利率溫和走升機率相對
較高，新興債市雖然多會受到升息壓力而出現一波
跌勢，但全年度仍有較大機率收紅 ，1996年以來僅
有2018、2022這兩個升息年度新興債市全年收黑 。
不過2022年新興債市重挫很大一部分原因來自於聯
準會在短短一年時間內將基準利率自0％升至4.25%，
這種猛爆性升息與目前所預設的風險管理式的溫和
升息環境有所不同；另外 ，2018年聯準會雖只升息
四碼，但土耳其及阿根廷因美元稀缺引發流動性風
險，這2國經常帳赤字占 GDP比重當時均高於 5%，
對外融資壓力上升的同時進而爆發金融危機 。然而
現階段新興國家經常帳已普遍改善 ，土耳其經常帳

赤字已降至 2%~3%，阿根廷更轉為順差 ，基本面
與2018年有很大的差異。若扣除這兩段期間，新興
債市在聯準會升息期間波動雖可能放大 ，但在貨幣
政策方向更趨明朗後 ，走勢便會止穩反彈 ，全年平
均報酬率仍有10%以上。
至於新興市場企業債部分 ，過去因升息所造成的跌
勢約是主權債的一半 ，主要是因為企業債發行天期
多較主權要來得短 ，加上該期間獲利動能強勁 ，因
此在利率上升時期表現往往相對主權債更具韌性 。
儘管利率走勢仍存不確定性，但最終若能穩定在5%
左右，預估新興企業債全年總報酬仍可維持在3%，
除非未來出現更大的外部衝擊 ，如利率持續大幅上
升引發對經濟成長受損的擔憂 ，或AI驅動的成長模
式被重新評價 ，否則對新興債市的看法仍將維持正
向，新興主要貨幣主權債在這樣的大環境下 ，短線
雖相對承壓，但後續反彈力道也可望大於企業債。
• 新興主要貨幣主權債在聯準
會升息年度正報酬機率近8成
• 新興企業債相對抗跌 ，公債
利率若穩定在5%左右，全年
總報酬仍可維持在3%
5
資料來源： BofA ICE Indices、Bloomberg，統計1996~2026/10/9，中國信託商業銀行，

今月の景気判断 ～持ち直している
【今月のポイント】
 東海経済は、持ち直している
 7月の生産（東海3県、季節調整値）は前月
比＋2.2％と2ヵ月ぶりに増加した。生産水
準も高く、増加基調にあるとみられる
 輸出は総じてみると足踏み状態。先行きは
持ち直しを見込むが、米・イランの対立長
期化などで足踏みとなるリスクがある
 日銀短観9月調査の業況判断DI（「良い」
－「悪い」）は、製造業が改善する一方、非
製造業は悪化した。設備投資も前年度比
増加が見込まれている
 8月の小売販売額は名目で2ヵ月連続で
前年比プラスとなったが、実質では3ヵ月
連続でマイナス。一方、新車販売台数は
5ヵ月連続で前年比プラスとなった

### Page 8

7 Mitsubishi UFJ Research and Consulting
個人消費（小売売上、自動車販売）
8月の小売販売額（名目）は前年比＋0.2％と小幅ながら2ヵ月連続でプラスとなった。実質値を試算する
と、前年比－2.0％と3ヵ月連続でマイナスとなった。
8月の新車販売は、前年比＋0.3％と5ヵ月連続で増加。軽自動車が減少したが、普通車、小型車が前
年を上回った。

## Document 569: 三星电子Q3营收再创新高，季度营业利润首次破百万亿韩元，但净利仍低于预期｜财报见闻

Source PDF: all report pdf/三星电子Q3营收再创新高，季度营业利润首次破百万亿韩元，但净利仍低于预期｜财报见闻.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

三星电⼦第三季度净利润 75.21 万亿韩元，预估 89.06 万亿韩元。三星电⼦第三季度营业利
润 107.44 万亿韩元。三星电⼦第三季度销售额 195.25 万亿韩元。三星第三季度芯⽚销售额
为 147.1 万亿韩元。三星将于 1 ⽉底公布股东回报计划，计划第三季度派发 30 万亿韩元现⾦
股息。
摘要：
在 AI 基础设施投资热潮推动下，三星电⼦的第三季度营收和营业利润再创新⾼，连续第四个季度
刷新纪录，季度营业利润⾸次突破 100 万亿韩元⼤关，但净利润表现不及市场预期。
10 ⽉ 29 ⽇，三星电⼦公布 2026 年第三季度详细财报，季度营收达 195.25 万亿韩元，同⽐增⻓
127% ，创历史新⾼，连续第四个季度刷新收⼊与利润纪录。营业利润达 107.44 万亿韩元，同⽐
暴增 783% ，环⽐增⻓ 20% ，营业利润率升⾄ 55% 。
不过，净利润表现不及市场预期。净利润为 75.21 万亿韩元，低于市场预估的 89.06 万亿韩元。
主要拖累来⾃⾮经营项⽬，第三季度财务费⽤为 9.5 万亿韩元，使得税前利润降⾄ 98.5 万亿韩

元；叠加 23.4 万亿韩元所得税，公司最终净利率为 38.5% ，较⼆季度的 41.8% 有所回落。
本轮业绩爆发的核⼼仍是半导体，尤其是 AI 相关存储产品。三星 DS 半导体业务第三季度销售额
达 147.1 万亿韩元，营业利润 106.5 万亿韩元，⼏乎贡献了集团全部营业利润。
存储业务销售额达到 140.7 万亿韩元，环⽐增⻓ 16% 、同⽐暴增 427% ，主要受 HBM4 放量、服务
器 SSD 需求强劲以及存储价格全⾯上涨推动。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

展望后续，三星给出更激进的资本开⽀计划：预计 2026 年资本⽀出为 84.4 万亿韩元，显著⾼于
2025 年的 52.7 万亿韩元，其中芯⽚资本⽀出约 76.8 万亿韩元，占⽐超过九成。
公司同时警告，随着 AI ⽣态继续扩张，芯⽚供应短缺问题明年将进⼀步加剧。
利润表：营业利润率升⾄ 55% ，但净利润被财务费⽤拖累
从整体盈利能⼒看，三星第三季度呈现典型的 AI 上⾏周期特征。
第三季度销售额 195.3 万亿韩元，较去年同期的 86.1 万亿韩元增⻓ 127% ；营业利润 107.4 万亿韩
元，较去年同期的 12.2 万亿韩元⼤增。公司称，这是全球科技公司⾸次实现单季营业利润超过
100 万亿韩元。
⽑利率⽅⾯，第三季度⽑利润为 135.3 万亿韩元，⽑利率 69.3% ，较去年同期的 38.9% ⼤幅提
升。营业利润率为 55.0% ，较⼆季度提升 2.8 个百分点，较去年同期的 14.1% 提升超过 40 个百分
点。
费⽤端相对克制。第三季度销售及管理费⽤为 27.8 万亿韩元，占营收⽐重 14.2% ，低于去年同期

的 24.8%；研发投⼊为 14.4 万亿韩元，环⽐下降 10% ，但同⽐仍增⻓ 63% ，显⽰三星在 AI 芯⽚、
先进制程和下⼀代显⽰等领域继续保持⾼投⼊。
但净利润没有同步超预期，关键在于财务费⽤。第三季度三星财务费⽤为负 9.5 万亿韩元，⽽⼆
季度为正 4.7 万亿韩元。受此影响，尽管营业利润创历史新⾼，税前利润为 98.5 万亿韩元，净利
润为 75.1 万亿韩元，低于市场预估。
半导体： HBM4 与服务器 SSD 成为业绩引擎
三星第三季度最核⼼的增⻓来源是 DS 半导体部⻔。
DS 半导体业务第三季度销售额 147.1 万亿韩元，环⽐增⻓ 15% ，同⽐增⻓ 344% ；营业利润
106.5 万亿韩元，较⼆季度增加 17.3 万亿韩元，较去年同期增加 99.5 万亿韩元。以分部收⼊计
算， DS 半导体营业利润率约 72% ，反映 AI 存储供不应求下的价格弹性。
其中，存储业务销售额 140.7 万亿韩元，环⽐增⻓ 16% ，同⽐增⻓ 427% 。三星表⽰，公司在产
能受限背景下主动优化产品组合，将资源集中投向 AI 相关产品；同时，服务器需求强劲推动各

应⽤领域存储价格上涨。

### Page 3

HBM4 是本季最重要的关键词。三星称，公司凭借产品竞争⼒显著扩⼤ HBM4 销售，并主动满⾜
服务器 SSD 需求增⻓。随着 AI 训练和推理集群继续扩张，⾼带宽内存、服务器 DRAM 以及企业
级 SSD 成为最紧俏产品。
三星对四季度也保持乐观。公司预计， AI 基础设施投资将继续受全球 AI 竞争推动，新⼀代
CPU/GPU 平台会带动存储需求进⼀步增⻓；与个⼈ AI Agent 服务扩张相关的 QLC SSD 需求也将
快速上升。
公司计划继续提⾼ HBM 、服务器 DRAM 和 SSD 销售，并扩⼤⾼速服务器 DDR5 及 QLC SSD 供
应。
晶圆代⼯与系统级芯⽚：先进制程需求回暖， Taylor ⼯⼚进⼊视野
除存储外，三星系统级芯⽚（ LSI ）和晶圆代⼯业务也出现改善。

### Page 4

系统级芯⽚⽅⾯，第三季度收⼊增⻓主要受益于下半年新机型中 SoC 和图像传感器量产。公司还
表⽰，通过建⽴新的客⼾合作关系，已获得定制 SoC 项⽬。四季度，三星预计旗舰 SoC 和 2 亿像
素传感器将维持稳定⽣产和销售。
晶圆代⼯业务则受益于先进节点需求。三星称，第三季度代⼯收⼊和利润显著改善，主要来⾃
HBM B-Die 销售增⻓以及先进制程销售扩⼤。公司还持续获得 2nm HPC 客⼾订单，其中包括⼤
型云服务商。
展望四季度，三星预计代⼯业务收⼊将实现双位数增⻓，并继续改善盈利，驱动因素包括 2nm
第⼆代移动新品、 4nm NPU 量产爬坡，以及存储相关产品销售扩⼤。
到 2027 年，三星将重点依托 4nm/2nm 先进节点承接 AI/HPC 需求，同时强化全球供给能⼒。公
司特别提到，美国 Taylor ⾸座晶圆⼚开始运营后，将增强其先进制程全球供应能⼒。
资本开⽀⼤幅上调：芯⽚投资占⽐超过九成
三星本次财报中最值得关注的前瞻信息，是资本开⽀计划的显著扩张。
公司预计 2026 年资本⽀出为 84.4 万亿韩元，相⽐ 2025 年的 52.7 万亿韩元增加约 60% ；其中芯⽚

资本⽀出约 76.8 万亿韩元，占总资本开⽀约 91% 。
这意味着三星正在将绝⼤部分投资资源投向半导体，尤其是 AI 存储、先进制程和服务器相关产
品。第三季度现⾦流量表也显⽰，公司购买物业、⼚房及设备⽀出为 26.9 万亿韩元，较⼆季度的
14.1 万亿韩元明显上升。
三星同时判断，随着 AI ⽣态扩张， AI 基础设施资本开⽀将在 2027 年继续加速， AI 驱动需求可能
令供应短缺程度较 2026 年进⼀步加剧。公司计划通过技术领先、产品组合优化和⻓期合同，锁
定⾼端市场需求。
资产负债表：净现⾦接近 200 万亿韩元，财务弹药充⾜
尽管资本开⽀⼤幅上⾏，三星资产负债表仍⾮常稳健。
截⾄ 9 ⽉底，公司总资产为 791.84 万亿韩元，较 6 ⽉底继续增⻓；现⾦及类现⾦资产为 214.82 万
亿韩元。同期债务为 17.19 万亿韩元，净现⾦达到 197.6 万亿韩元，⾼于 6 ⽉底的 167.6 万亿韩
元。
杠杆⽔平继续较低。三星负债权益⽐为 29% ，债务权益⽐仅 3% ，净债务权益⽐为 -32% ；流动⽐

率为 310% ，较去年同期的 263% 进⼀步提⾼。
现⾦流⽅⾯，第三季度经营活动现⾦流为 98.6 万亿韩元，低于⼆季度的 105.1 万亿韩元但仍处⾼
位。投资活动现⾦流为 -35.2 万亿韩元，其中购买物业、⼚房及设备⽀出 26.9 万亿韩元；融资活
动现⾦流为 -24.0 万亿韩元，主要包括 12.5 万亿韩元库存股回购、 2.5 万亿韩元股息⽀付以及债务
减少 4.6 万亿韩元。
股东回报：三季度现⾦股息 30 万亿韩元，未来回购或向优先股倾斜
三星同步更新了股东回报计划。
公司计划第三季度派发总额 30 万亿韩元现⾦股息，普通股和优先股每股均为 4601 韩元。

### Page 5

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

1 条评论
按照公司披露， 2026 年预计总股东回报规模为 90 万亿⾄ 110 万亿韩元，其中 35 万亿韩元已计划
或完成，剩余 55 万亿⾄ 75 万亿韩元将通过现⾦股息以及⼤规模股票回购并注销等⽅式返还。
值得注意的是，三星特别回应了市场对普通股与优先股价差的关注。公司表⽰，将在未来股票
回购和注销计划中，根据市场情况积极考虑并调整优先股占⽐。
三星称，下⼀轮股东回报政策，以及 2026 年剩余股东回报的最终⾦额和具体执⾏计划，将在
2027 年 1 ⽉底董事会会议上最终确定并公布。

## Document 571: 中东石油出口量“已超过战前”，但油价依然高企，“4月短缺的是原油，10月则是运输成本”

Source PDF: all report pdf/中东石油出口量“已超过战前”，但油价依然高企，“4月短缺的是原油，10月则是运输成本”.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-28
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

波斯湾⽯油出⼝量已超过战前⽔平，但油价依然⾼企，布伦特原油仍报约每桶 108 美元，本
⽉约 30 起袭击事件推动运费逼近纪录⾼位。摩根⼤表⽰： “4 ⽉短缺的是原油， 10 ⽉则是运
输成本。 ” 超级油轮运往某亚洲⼤国的运费接近每桶 37.37 美元的纪录⾼位。
摘要：
波斯湾⽯油出⼝已恢复⾄战前⽔平以上，但⾼昂的运输成本和持续的袭击事件，令市场对这⼀
局⾯能否持续⼼存疑虑。
据船舶追踪机构 Kpler 数据，截⾄ 10 ⽉ 18 ⽇当周，中东⽯油⽇均流量（含霍尔⽊兹海峡及区域替
代航线）超过 2400 万桶，⾼于战前的 2330 万桶。
与此同时，布伦特原油本周⼆报约每桶 108 美元，近期持续⾼位运⾏。摩根⼤通全球⼤宗商品策
略团队负责⼈ Natasha Kaneva 对此直⾔： "4 ⽉短缺的是原油， 10 ⽉则是运输成本。 "
袭击升级，运输成本逼近纪录
据英国海上贸易⾏动中⼼及多家海事安全公司报告的统计，本⽉已有约 30 起船只遭袭事件。海
事情报公司 Windward ⾼级分析师 Michelle Wiese Bockmann 指出， 10 ⽉的袭击事件占整个战争

期间被袭船只总数的 20% 。
Kpler 数据显⽰，本周超级油轮从海湾运往某亚洲⼤国的运费接近每桶 37.37 美元的纪录⾼位。据
Windward 的 Wiese Bockmann ，战争⻛险保险费率已从不⾜船只价值的 3% 攀升⾄最⾼ 10% 。
坦桑尼亚籍超级油轮 "Loyalty" 号的遭遇，是这场⾼⻛险博弈的缩影。该船 9 ⽉起两度进⼊波斯湾
装载伊拉克原油，船员表⽰公司为第⼆次航⾏配发了防弹⾐和头盔。 10 ⽉ 16 ⽇， "Loyalty" 号在
运载 200 万桶伊拉克原油途中，遭⼀艘⼩艇发射的⽕箭推进榴弹击中，弹头在淡⽔舱上炸出⼀个
洞。
Neptune P2P Group 前英国海军军官 Christopher Long 表⽰： " 伊朗正试图重新封锁霍尔⽊兹海
峡。 "
出⼝量回升，但霍尔⽊兹仍未全⾯恢复
Kpler 初步数据显⽰，同期霍尔⽊兹海峡⽯油⽇均流量（含阿曼湾海上转运）约为 1400 万桶，相
当于战前⽔平的 82% 。
沙特阿拉伯和阿联酋的跨境管道正在分担部分压⼒。 Kpler 估计， 9 ⽉份从海湾地区出⼝的⾮伊朗

原油中，约 40% 绕开了霍尔⽊兹海峡，⽽战前这⼀⽐例仅为 17% 。
Capital Economics ⾼级⼤宗商品经济学家 Hamad Hussain 表⽰： " 整体来看，在危险的霍尔⽊兹
海峡进⾏航运的体系已经相当成熟，参与⽯油出⼝的各⽅⻛险承受意愿也有所上升。 "
暴利驱动，船东和船员冒险⼊局
⾼⻛险背后是⾼回报。

湾，以捕捉该地区异常⾼企的运费⾏情。

## Document 616: 凱基投顧_3324 雙鴻_向子慧_20261021

Source PDF: all report pdf/凱基投顧_3324 雙鴻_向子慧_20261021.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-21
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

十月 21, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新  散熱 ‧ 台灣

雙鴻 
(3324.TW /3324 TT)

新專案與客戶訂單增加，推升液冷營收成長 
重要訊息  
雙鴻 3Q26 營收低於預期，主因泰國 水災及部分專案出貨遞延至 4Q26 。然
液冷業務營收持續成長，預期將帶動 4Q26 至 2027 年 EPS 維持強勁成長。  
評論及分析  
出貨遞延至  4Q26 ；3Q26 營收低於預期。 雙鴻 3Q26 營收季增僅  13%，低
於公司指引及我們預估的  15-20% 成長，主因泰國淹水 及專案出貨遞延， 對 
3Q26 營收的影響約為  4-5 億元，約占 4-5%。由於 訂單未變 ，我們預期 營
收遞延 將帶動 4Q26 營收季增超過  20%。儘管 3Q26 營收低於預期，伺服
器營收比重仍持續提升至  81% ，高於  2Q26 的 78% 。液冷營收占比亦由  
2Q26 及 1H26 的 55% 提升至 3Q26 的 66%，產品組合改善，有利於伺服
器毛利率。然而，由於  PC 與 VGA 營收及產能利用率明顯下滑，預估  3Q26 
毛利率 僅小幅 季增 至約 29.5%，而預估 4Q26 營收強勁將推升毛利率至
30% 。我們據此調整  2026 年營收預估至年增  67% ，主要成長動 能來自伺
服器業務，營收年增  130-140%。我們將  2026 年 EPS 預估調整為  55.01 
元，年增  95%。 
液冷 專案斬獲將 推升  2027 年 營收。 雙鴻  1Q–3Q26 液冷營收 年增  290-
300% ，營收占比提升至  59%，高於  1Q–3Q25 的 26% 。成長動能主要來
自 AI 伺服器需求，雙鴻主力產品為水冷板及內部／機櫃分歧管， 同時有 泰
國 及 中 國 產 能 擴 充 支 撐 。我 們 預 估 液 冷 營 收 今 年 將 年 增約 200% ，並於  
2027 年成長 80-100% ，主要受惠於對  AWS ( 美) 的 ASIC 出貨增加，以 及
對多家 CSP 的分歧管及  CDU 銷售增加。 2H27 潛在新增  CDU 客戶為另一 動
能。而水冷板營收主要 來自  HGX 的 DIMM 與 PCIe ，部分來自  GB ／VR 
Compute Tray 。我們預估  Trainium 3 Max (T3 Max) ( 液冷) 出貨將於  11 月
後量產，而公司可望 2027 年 T3 液冷供應份額 約 20-25%，額外 貢獻營收
約 70-80 億元 (15-20%) 。AWS Trainium 4 目標全面採用液冷設計，相較  
T3 約 40% ，將為 2028 年水冷營收的強勁驅 動力。隨液冷 營收占比預期升
至 2026 年的 62% 、2027 年的 70-75% 及 2028 年的逾 80%，我們預估
毛 利 率 將 由 今 年 的 29.4% 提 升 至 明 年 的 30.2% ， 同 期 營 業 利 益 率 則 由 
17.7% 升至 20.3% 。我們微調  2026 年 EPS 預估至 55.01 元 (年增 95%)，
但上調 2027 年 EPS 至 99.35 元 (年增 81%)。 
投資建議  
我們維持雙鴻「增加持股」評等，因液冷營收增加將推升利潤率與  EPS 成
長，目標價由  1,410 元調升至  1,950 元 (21 倍 2027 年完全稀釋  EPS) 。 
投資風險  
原料價格上漲；筆電、顯卡或伺服器需求疲弱。

圖 8：公司概況    圖 9：1-3Q26 年伺服器 營收比重 升至 78%  
雙鴻成立於 1998 年，為台灣前三大散熱模組廠商，主要業務
涵蓋筆電 / 桌機、顯示卡 / 電競筆電、伺服器、智慧手機。主要
客戶為微星 (2377 TT , NT$153,持有)、Dell( 美)、HP( 美)、華碩  
(235 7 TT , NT$982, 增加持股 )、Apple (美)。2025 年營收組合
為筆電&桌電 27%、顯示卡 / 電競 PC 14%、伺服器 57%、其
他 2%。公司 2026-27 年主要營收成長動能 為伺服器。

## Document 630: 凱基投顧_Takeaway_3105 穩懋_徐雋翔_20261028

### Page 1

十月 28, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 1

法說會重點摘要

穩懋 
(3105 TT, NT$ 540, 未評等)

重點摘要  
1. 穩懋 3Q26 合併營收 58.59 億元，QoQ 11% 、YoY 31% ；受惠產
品組合轉向航太衛星與光通，合併毛利率 37.4% ， QoQ 
+9.2ppts ，創 18 個季度新高，個體毛利率 40.7% 睽違 18 個季度
重回 40%以上；營業利益率 24.2% ，加上持股 23%的聯茂權益法
收益大增， EPS 4.55 元，QoQ 98% 。Infrastructure 營收占比升至
35-40%，與 Optical 合計已超過一半  
2. 公司預期 4Q26 合併營收 QoQ 成長 low single digit ，合併毛利率
維持 high-30s；年底歲修、 Cellular 淡季與記憶體缺貨為主要壓抑
因素，衛星通訊新規格導入與光通產能到位則提供支撐。 PD 已於
2Q-3Q26 量產並逐季放量， 2027 年將開出大部分產能 
3. 光通雷射方面，CW Laser 與 EML 仍在客戶 reliability 驗證，2027
年為量產前少量出貨， 2028 年才有較大量貢獻； GaN 預計 2027
年導入 6 吋製程。我們認為， 3Q26 毛利率躍升主要來自高毛利的
Infrastructure 占比提升，而非稼動率回升，獲利結構正由手機旺季
驅動轉向衛星與光通  
結論 
我們看好穩懋獲利結構轉型已進入兌現期。我們認為， 3Q26 合併毛利
率跳升至 37.4% 以產品組合優化為主因，公司亦表示稼動率越接近
80% 以上對毛利率的影響越小；而 4Q26 在手機淡季與年底歲修下，
合併毛利率仍可維持 high- 30s ，顯示毛利率新水準具延續性。後續驗
證點為 4Q26 毛利率能否守穩，以及下一次法說提出的 2027 年全年展
望；PD 於 2027 年開出大部分產能、低軌衛星規格升級與 D2C 布建，
加上 2028 年 CW Laser 與 EML 放量，將為穩懋中長期成長提供動能

3Q26 營運成果  
 3Q26 合併營收 58.59 億元，QoQ 11% 、YoY 31% ，符合公司預
期；成長來自傳統旺季，以及銷售組合轉向航太衛星與光通兩項應
用 
 合併毛利率 37.4% ，QoQ +9.2ppts ，創 18 個季度新高；個體毛利
率 40.7% ，QoQ +8.1ppts ，睽違 18 個季度重回 40%，公司表示
毛利已回到庫存調整前水準 
 產能利用率由 2Q26 的 65%升至 75%，但毛利率提升主要來自
產品組合優化  
 營業費用率 13% 大致持平，營業利益 14.16 億元，QoQ 91% 、
YoY 267% ，營業利益率 24.2% ，QoQ +10.1ppts

階 Android 與 iOS 市場，仍具相對優勢  
 Wi-Fi 占比 15-20%，因 2Q26 基期較低與 router 訂單回流，QoQ
雙位數成長

開出，產銷量上升

high- 30s；指引為合併口徑，與 3Q26 的 37.4% 為同一水準 
 壓抑因素：年底例行歲修、 Cellular 進入淡季，以及記憶體缺貨
造成的終端漲價干擾  
 支撐因素：航太與衛星通訊終端新規格導入、光通專案產能持續
到位 
 產能利用率預計維持 70%以上；折舊與 3Q26 的 7.06 億元相近，
因設備陸續到位但尚未資本化；2026 年資本支出約 20 億元，主要
用於機器設備

VCSEL 、CW Laser 、EML 及 InP PIC ，CW Laser 朝 200-400mW 高

台灣 穩懋

 PD 已於 2Q-3Q26 量產，出貨逐季提升，4Q26 預計較 3Q26 有不

 4 吋產線不只生產 PD，各類 4 吋 InP 產品皆可切換，產能並未滿
載 
 CW Laser 與 EML 已有客戶完成大部分產品開發，仍在進行
reliability 驗證，單次週期即需半年  
 2027 年為量產前少量出貨， 2028 年才有較大量營收貢獻 
 GaN 產能目前皆為 4 吋並持續擴充，預計 2027 年將 6 吋 GaN 製
程正式導入給客戶； pHEMT 與 GaN 製程由市場主流的 0.25-
0.15 μm 推進至 0.1μm ，搶攻新世代衛星通訊  
 InP 基板供應方面，穩懋以 process 代工為主，大量客戶多帶已完成
磊晶的晶圓投片，需穩懋代做磊晶的大客戶亦能自行確保基板供
應，目前未見影響

QA 
 低軌衛星營收占比多少？是否集中單一客戶？  
 衛星與長距離 PtP 技術高度重疊，部分訂單亦經既有 PtP 客戶進
來，無法精準拆分，不單獨揭露低軌衛星占比  
 穩懋耕耘衛星通訊逾 10 年，高、中、低軌道衛星皆不只一個客
戶，近期成長多來自量較大的低軌衛星  
 低軌衛星規格自 2026 年起大幅提升，加上未來 D2C 全面布建，
公司對 2027 年需求維持相對樂觀 
 3Q26 毛利率大幅提升的主因？ 
 Infrastructure 貢獻最大，該產品線毛利率相當高，營收占比較
2Q26 增加 5ppts 以上 
 稼動率提升的貢獻有限，產能利用率越接近 80% 以上對毛利率
影響越小，毛利率提升絕大多數來自產品組合優化  
 Optical 仍為前期少量量產，大幅貢獻在 2027 年 
 4Q26 毛利率是否較 3Q26 下滑？ 
 4Q26 high -30s 指引為合併毛利率，與 3Q26 合併毛利率 37.4%
在同一水準，並無衰退；不宜與個體毛利率 40.7% 比較 
 持股 23%的聯茂如何認列？ 
 採權益法依聯茂獲利認列 23% ，列入業外收入，與聯茂股價波
動無關 
 2027 年營運與折舊展望？  
 2027 年手機機種尚未定案，公司需彙整更多客戶資訊，將於下
一次法說給出較明確方向，本場未提供全年指引  
 2027 年折舊須待預算編列完成、確認設備轉入資本化金額後才
會提供範圍

## Document 666: 在“ChatGPT时刻”到来前，宇树科技不急着赚钱

Source PDF: all report pdf/在“ChatGPT时刻”到来前，宇树科技不急着赚钱.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

宇树科技 Q3 营收同⽐增约 70% ， G1 ⼈形机器⼈已成全球销量第⼀。但公司正主动以利润换
规模：研发⽀出同⽐暴增约 250% ，⽑利率承压，经营性现⾦流同⽐下降约 55% 。摩根⼤通
认为在物理 AI“ChatGPT 时刻 ” 到来前，宇树的核⼼价值在于出货量、数据和⽣态的持续积
累，⽽⾮短期盈利，并维持增持评级。
摘要：
宇树科技正在⽤利润换未来。
三季度营收同⽐增⻓ 70% ， G1 ⼈形机器⼈跻⾝全球最畅销机型 ——这家机器⼈公司交出了⼀份
亮眼的成绩单。但与此同时，研发费⽤单季度暴增 250% ，⽑利率承压，经营现⾦流⼤幅收缩。
据追⻛交易台消息，摩根⼤通 10 ⽉ 29 ⽇的研报指出，当前市场的争论焦点，" 与其说是即期利润
率扩张，不如说是宇树科技能否持续抢占份额、积累数据、巩固⽣态系统领先地位 " 。
这是⼀家在物理 AI"ChatGPT 时刻 " 真正到来之前，主动选择不急着赚钱的公司。摩根⼤通在最新
研报中维持对宇树科技的 " 增持 " 评级，⽬标价 995 元，较当前股价 695.90 元（ 10 ⽉ 28 ⽇收盘）有

约 43% 的上⾏空间。
出货量全⾯提速， G1 成全球最畅销⼈形机器⼈
据研报，宇树科技三季度出货量增⻓强劲，覆盖双⾜和四⾜平台，涵盖⼤⼩尺⼨机型，增⻓已
呈全⾯扩张态势。
G1 ⼈形机器⼈⽬前是全球最畅销机型，累计出货量在 1 万⾄ 2 万台区间。四⾜机器⼈同样保持全
球领先，⼩型机器⼈在教育和科研领域持续渗透，⼤型机器⼈则向⼯业和公共服务场景延伸。
前三季度累计营收约 18 亿元，同⽐增⻓约 56% ；三季度单季营收约 6.65 亿元，同⽐增⻓约
70% 。
该⾏分析师指出，这⼀出货量的扩张，背后是越来越多真实落地部署，⽽⾮仅停留在试点阶段
—— 这正是公司数据积累和反馈闭环的核⼼驱动⼒。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

研发⽀出暴增，利润承压是主动选择
三季度，宇树研发⽀出同⽐增⻓约 252% ，⾄约 1.3 亿元；前三季度累计约 2.6 亿元。
分析师指出，这部分增加的研发⽀出，不仅⽤于产品开发，还涵盖具⾝智能、场景覆盖和真实
测试基础设施，以及物理 AI 开发和算⼒⽀出 —— 这与宇树在⽣态活动上释放的信号⼀致：公司对
物理 AI 进展的判断正在变得更加积极。
与此同时，⽑利率受到物料成本上涨、持续的价格竞争以及研发和⽣态投⼊加⼤的三重压⼒。
管理层正与供应商合作优化成本结构，但摩根⼤通认为，近期⽑利率压⼒⼤概率将持续，因为
公司当前明确将⽣态建设置于短期盈利之上。
值得注意的是，宇树⽬前仍维持盈利状态。前三季度净利润约 3.87 亿元，同⽐增⻓约 272% 。摩
根⼤通将这⼀点视为其在机器⼈整机⼚商中的关键差异化优势 —— 在激进扩张模式下依然保持
盈利，在同⾏中属于少数。
现⾦流收窄，资本开⽀三倍增⻓仍可⾃给
规模扩张的代价也体现在现⾦流上。
前三季度，宇树经营性现⾦流约为 1.9 亿元，同⽐下降约 55% ，主要原因是营运资本需求增加，

以及在库存、⼈员和技术上的持续投⼊。
资本开⽀⽅⾯，前三季度约为 1 亿元，同⽐增⻓约两倍，主要⽤于扩⼤产能、升级研发基础设施
和技术投资。分析师指出，尽管资本开⽀⼤幅增⻓，但在同期仍可实现⾃给，⽆需外部融资。
⽣态系统⻜轮加速，开发者⼤会聚集逾 3000 ⼈
宇树科技近期举办的⽣态系统活动吸引了超过 3000 名参与者，涵盖城市治理、应急响应、教
育、娱乐和⼯业应⽤等多个论坛与展览。
公司还投⼊百万级奖⾦举办全球竞赛，⾯向全球团队和个⼈开放，赛道包括运动竞技、格⽃、
灵巧操作和医疗健康。
分析师写道，这些活动 " 并⾮只是展⽰ —— 它们的设计⽬的是增加场景多样性，产⽣边缘案例，
加速仿真、真实测试和模型改进之间的反馈循环 " 。
结果是，宇树科技正在成为模型公司、⾼校和系统集成商寻求真实数据、测试和部署接触点时
的默认合作伙伴。
摩根⼤通维持增持，⽬标价 995 元
摩根⼤通维持宇树科技 " 增持 " 评级，⽬标价为 995 元（基于 2028 年预期市销率 30 倍），较当前股
价（ 695.9 元）有约 43% 的上⾏空间。

分析师指出，⾃ 10 ⽉以来，宇树科技股价已累计上涨约 55% ，⼤幅跑赢沪深 300 指数同期约 -6%
的表现。

### Page 3

分析师认为， " 物理 AI 销售的 S 曲线很可能正在被加速拉前 " ，市场将聚焦于销售爬坡的速度与可
持续性，以及试点项⽬向重复企业订单的转化能⼒。
下⾏⻛险包括：企业采购进展慢于预期、竞争加剧侵蚀定价权，以及监管收紧或地缘政治⻛险
限制海外扩张。

策略 
短期的风险事件正在逐渐的落地，关注未来不确定性降低后的市场机会。今日美联储宣布10月议息会议结果，显
示美联储在上调经济判断 （从稳健转向强劲） 的背景下维持政策的按兵不动 （从实现2%的目标转向积累更多信息） ，
政策有转“鸽”的倾向。继续等待11月美国中期选举事件的落地。

## Document 765: 海外宏观周报：全球主要债市收益率普遍下行

Source PDF: all report pdf/海外宏观周报：全球主要债市收益率普遍下行.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

··
关注东方金诚公众号
获取更多研究报告
作者
时间
东方金诚 研究发展部
分析师 徐嘉琦
执行总监 于丽峰
执行总监 冯琳
2026 年 10 月 21 日
全球主要债市收益率普遍下行
——海外宏观周报（2026.10.12-2026.10.18）
核心观点
 全球：上周美联储主席沃什发言偏鸽，美债收益率大幅
下行，全球主要债市收益率普遍走低，其中，10 年期美
债收益率大幅下行 15bp 至 5.09%，英债、德债、法债、
日债收益率分别下行 10.2bp、5bp、7.3bp 和 13.1bp。
上周全球股市剧烈分化，A股上证跌2.6%、深成指跌6.6%，
韩国综指暴跌 14.5%，日经跌 5.4%；欧股逆势上涨（英
国+3.1%、德国+2.1%、法国+2.0%），美股表现分化。上
周 WTI 原油价格大涨 9.4%至 99 美元/桶，美元指数微跌
0.4%至 101.78，黄金价格基本持平于 4189 美元/盎司。
 美国：上周美联储主席沃什发言未对利率路径给出指引，
市场10月加息概率一度跌破15%；但9月CPI同比3.7%、

核心 CPI2.6%，双双超预期，PPI 同比 6.3%，创四年最
高，数据公布后 10 月加息概率再度升温。财政方面，上
周公布的 2026 财年赤字达 1.99 万亿美元（同比+12%），
净利息支出首破 1 万亿美元至 1.05 万亿，占税收收入超
五分之一，CBO 预计利息支出或于 2028 年超越社保。
 全球资金流向：上周全球股票基金净流入 85.14 亿美元，
债券基金净流入 5.86 亿美元，较前周边际减少。上周股
票基金净流入规模居前的市场为美国、中国、中国台湾，
债券基金净流入规模居前的市场为美国、中国、加拿大。

1
一、政策动向
1.货币政策
沃什发言“偏鸽”，市场押注美联储 10 月按兵不动，加息概率跌破 15%。10 月 16 日，
美联储主席沃什在国际货币基金组织年会的一场对话活动中，对货币政策前景几乎未作具体
表态，面对市场对加息路径的关切，沃什发言刻意保持克制，仅重申美联储致力于实现价格
稳定，“我们已明确表示将实现价格稳定，并在约一个月前撤回了一定程度的宽松政策”。
他表示，“我不会预判会议结果，我不认为现在应该说出我在今年剩余会议上可能采取的行
动。”沃什同时表示，自 9 月 FOMC 会议以来，“整体经济形势大体符合我们的预期”。这一
偏鸽姿态令投资者迅速调整押注——市场对美联储10月27日至28日政策会议加息的概率预
期，从沃什发言前的约 27%骤降至 15%以下。
美联储理事鲍曼：不认为联储迫切需要加息。10 月 14 日，美联储理事、负责监管的副
主席鲍曼在曼谷举行的国际金融协会（IIF）年度会员峰会上表示，在 9 月会议决定加息后，

联储当前无需急于采取进一步货币政策行动，决策者有时间评估经济走势再做决策。她承认
通胀仍高于美联储目标，但强调应全面评估经济数据，观察金融市场与经济形势如何演变，
再考虑下一步行动。上述言论与近期多位美联储官员的基调一致，但内部分歧依然存在，部
分官员仍倾向于年内再度加息一次。
2.财政政策
美国 2026 财年赤字 1.99 万亿美元，增长 12%，利息支出或于 2028 年超越社保。10 月
16 日，美国财政部公布的数据显示，截至 9 月的 2026 财年联邦预算赤字扩大至 1.99 万亿美
元，较上一财年的 1.78 万亿美元增加约 12%；支出增长约 6%至 7.4 万亿美元，收入增长约
3%至 5.4 万亿美元，收支缺口持续拉大；利息支出首次突破万亿美元门槛，净利息支出增加
814 亿美元至1.05 万亿美元，创历史新高，成为本财年赤字扩大的重要推手。此外，特朗普
政府 2025 年减税法案导致企业所得税收入下降逾 700 亿美元，进一步削弱财政收入基础。财
政部长贝森特将关税退款列为赤字扩大的关键因素之一，这一退款源于最高法院今年 2 月裁

定废除特朗普第二任期内大部分关税加征措施。

二、经济数据
1.美国
美国 9 月 PPI 同比上涨 6.3%，创四年来最高纪录，核心涨幅扩大至 5%。10 月 15 日，美
国劳工统计局公布的数据显示，美国 9 月 PPI 同比上涨 6.3%，远超市场预期，创 2022 年 12
月以来最高纪录；环比上涨 1.3%，为 2022 年 3 月以来最大单月涨幅，同样大幅超出预期。
与此同时，9 月剔除食品和能源的核心 PPI 同比上涨 5.0%，高于预期值 4.4%及前值 4.6%，环
比上涨 0.7%，亦超出预期。9 月 PPI 大幅走高，能源价格是核心驱动力，当月能源 PPI 环比
上涨 10.8%。此外，9 月运输与仓储价格环比上涨 3.4%，食品价格环比上涨 1.2%，多个分项
均呈现较强的价格压力。能源成本的快速传导，折射出供应链各环节所承受的成本压力，并
预示着通胀向下游消费端进一步渗透的风险。此次 PPI 数据发布于 CPI 超预期报告后的次日，
两份报告共同指向通胀压力的顽固性。

美国 9 月零售环比增 0.8%，剔除波动项后消费增长乏力。10 月 15 日，美国商务部公布
的数据显示，9 月零售销售环比增长 0.8%，高于市场预期的 0.3%，同比增幅达 6.3%，但剔除
汽车及汽油后的核心零售销售仅增长 0.2%，直接反映 GDP 商品消费计算的“控制组”销售额
环比仅增 0.1%，较 8 月大幅回落，显示消费动能在第三季度末出现明显回落。9 月零售销售
增长的主要贡献来自加油站，该品类销售额环比飙升 6.4%，为 13 个品类中涨幅最大。此外，
在燃油支出加速增长的同时，9 月多个主要消费品类出现软化，包括家居改善（-1.6%）、百
货商场（-1.1%）、服装（-0.9%）、家具（-0.5%）、餐饮（-0.4%）、食品杂货（-0.2%）以
及线上消费（-0.1%）。
美国 9 月 CPI 同比涨 3.7%、核心CPI 涨 2.6%，双双超预期。10 月 14 日，美国劳工统计
局公布的数据显示，9 月 CPI 同比上涨 3.7%，高于市场预期值 3.6%和前值 3.4%；核心 CPI同

比上涨 2.6%，同样超出预期的 2.5%。环比方面，美国 9 月CPI 环比上涨 0.6%，与预期持平，
前值为 0.4%；核心 CPI 环比上涨 0.3%，高于预期的 0.2%，与前值持平。此次数据是美联储
10月27日至28日利率决议前最后一份CPI报告，其结果将直接影响决策层的利率路径判断。
数据发布后，交易员大幅上调对美联储 10 月加息的押注，10 月加息概率飙升至 50%，同时预

## Document 776: 瑞银再度下调韩股目标位！汇率与盈利预期承压，2027年有望复苏

Source PDF: all report pdf/瑞银再度下调韩股目标位！汇率与盈利预期承压，2027年有望复苏.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

瑞银年内⼆度下调韩国 KOSPI ⽬标价，从 8000 点压低⾄ 7500 点。韩元升值 15% 叠加 EPS
持续下修，外资 10 ⽉净卖出逾 190 亿韩元构成主要抛压。但瑞银强调，这是汇率驱动的 " 盈
利重置 " ⽽⾮下⾏周期，看好 2027 年复苏，上⾏⽬标剑指 9300 点。
摘要：
继今年 9 ⽉下调之后，瑞银再度下调对韩国股市的 12 个⽉⽬标位，将 KOSPI ⽬标价由此前的
8000 点下调 6% ⾄ 7500 点，对应远期市盈率 6 倍这是的⼜⼀次调整。
据追⻛交易台，瑞银在 10 ⽉ 29 ⽇报告中指出，此次下调的核⼼驱动⼒在于汇率与⾃下⽽上的盈
利修正，宏观经济压⼒⽇益加剧将拖累每股盈利，同时⽬前股市尚未充分反映韩元升值的完整
冲击，
近期 KOSPI 已从⾼点回落约 10% ，逼近其下⾏价值区间 5000 点（对应 4 倍市盈率）， KOSPI 200
看跌 / 看涨⽐率触及负⼀个标准差，历史上这⼀信号往往预⽰市场超卖。瑞银认为，当前市场⻛
险厌恶情绪已过度，在不出现持续盈利下⾏或外⽣冲击的前提下，进⼀步下⾏空间有限。

展望后市，瑞银预计 KOSPI 在年底前仍将承压，但看好 2027 年的复苏空间，⽀撑因素包括强劲
的每股盈利（ EPS ）增⻓、股东回报改善、流动性约束缓解以及加息周期趋于尾声。瑞银将上
⾏⽬标位设定为 9,300 点（对应 8 倍市盈率）。
汇率与盈利双重夹击
瑞银对 KOSPI 的⽬标位调整已是今年第⼆次。今年 9 ⽉ 18 ⽇，瑞银分析师 Yong-Suk Son 将
KOSPI 12 个⽉⽬标价从 8,800 点下调⾄ 8,000 点，降幅近 10% ，理由是利率上⾏、韩元⾛强及油
价攀升带来的宏观逆⻛。彼时，瑞银指出 10 年期国债收益率已从年初的 3.4% 跳升⾄ 4.5% ，韩国
央⾏⾃ 7 ⽉以来两度加息，油价亦突破每桶 100 美元。
时隔约六周，瑞银再度出⼿，将⽬标位进⼀步压低⾄ 7,500 点。此次下调的核⼼驱动⼒在于汇率
与⾃下⽽上的 EPS 修正。瑞银分析显⽰，韩元⾃ 6 ⽉以来升值约 15% ，若其他条件不变，将拖累
2026 年和 2027 年 KOSPI 每股盈利分别约 2% 和 10%。与此同时， 10 ⽉份市场共识 EPS ⽉度修正

幅度仅为 -0.5% ，尚未充分反映韩元升值的完整冲击，意味着后续仍存在进⼀步下修压⼒。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

从⾏业层⾯看，化⼯、娱乐、国防、汽⻋、可选消费、多元⾦融、互联⽹及化妆品板块在 2026
年和 2027 年预期中均遭遇最⼤幅度的盈利下调；能源、运输、建筑及保险板块则相对获得上
调。
外资持续净卖出，市场情绪触及极端
资⾦流向数据揭⽰了近期市场承压的另⼀⾯。 10 ⽉以来，外资净卖出规模⾼达 190.0 亿韩元，成
为市场最主要的抛压来源，部分由企业回购（ 99.0 亿韩元）、散⼾（ 58.0 亿韩元）及本地机构
（ 32.0 亿韩元）承接。
不过，瑞银指出资⾦⾯已出现边际改善迹象：外资净卖出规模近期有所收窄，本周甚⾄转为净
买⼊；散⼾在过去两周转为净卖出，⽽企业回购在 SK 海⼒⼠（ SK Hynix ）重启回购计划后有望
获得⽀撑；养⽼⾦虽仍处于观望，但若 KOSPI 进⼀步接近下⾏价值区间，其资产配置⽬标或将
触发⼊场。
从估值与情绪指标来看， KOSPI 当前以 1.1 倍 NTM 市净率交易，与历史均值基本持平，股息收益
率达 2.7% （处于历史均值正⼀个标准差），提供⼀定估值⽀撑。剔除三星电⼦和 SK 海⼒⼠后，
KOSPI 的 NTM 市净率仅为 0.9 倍（负 0.6 个标准差）， NTM 市盈率为 6.6 倍（负 2.6 个标准差），估
值已处于历史偏低⽔平。
盈利重置⽽⾮下⾏周期， 2027 年复苏可期
尽管短期承压，瑞银对韩国股市的中期前景维持相对乐观。瑞银预测 2026 年 KOSPI 每股盈利增
速为 +258% （市场共识为 +278% ）， 2027 年为 +33% （市场共识为 +38% ），其预测已将汇率压⼒
纳⼊考量，较市场共识更为保守。
瑞银强调，当前的 EPS 下调更多是汇率驱动的 " 重置 " ，⽽⾮盈利持续下⾏周期的开端。科技板块
仍是 2026 年盈利增⻓的最⼤贡献来源， 2027 年电池材料、公⽤事业及化⼯板块有望接棒领涨。

## Document 782: 米インフレの動向－関税・エネルギー・AI投資による物価上昇を検証

Report source: NLI
Date: 2026-10-23

（資料）労働統計局（BLS）よりニッセイ基礎研究所作成
１. 米国の26年 9月の消費者物価指数（ＣＰＩ）は、総合指数が前年同月比+3.7％（前月：
+3.4％）と再加速。エネルギー価格の上昇が押し上げたほか、コア指数（食料品・エネ
ルギーを除く）も同+2.6％（前月：+2.4％）と小幅に加速。一方、コア財価格の伸びは
限定的。 
２. 関税は25年春以降のコア財価格を押し上げたものの、実効関税率の低下などから影響は
当初想定を下回った。今後は価格転嫁の一巡や前年のベース効果の剥落に伴い、既存関
税による押し上げ効果は減衰する見込み。ただし、追加関税による物価上昇リスクは残
る。 
３. 一方、中東情勢の緊迫化に伴う原油・ガソリン価格の上昇が、足元のインフレ再加速の
主因となっている。エネルギー価格の上昇は総合ＣＰＩを直接押し上げるほか、輸送費
や生産コストを通じて他の財・サービス価格に波及する可能性がある。 
４. また、ＡＩ関連投資に伴う電力需要拡大も、電力料金の上昇要因として注目される。電
力料金は26年9月に前年同月比+4.4％（前月：+3.8％）と加速。もっとも、燃料費や送
配電投資なども影響するため、ＡＩ関連投資による押し上げ効果は慎重な評価が必要。 
５. 当研究所では、ＣＰＩ上昇率を26年が前年比+3.4％、27年が同+2.5％と予想。26年は
エネルギー価格の上昇などから前年の+2.6％を上回る一方、27年は既存関税の押し上げ
効果の減衰などから鈍化する見込み。ただし、エネルギー価格や追加関税、電力料金な
どが上振れリスクとなろう。 
ニッセイ基礎研究所 2026-10-23 
米インフレの動向 
－関税・エネルギー・AI投資による物価上昇を検証

経済研究部 主任研究員 窪谷 浩 
(03)3512-1824  kubotani @nli-research.co.jp

### Page 2

２． 足元のインフレ動向と物価押し上げ要因 
（ＣＰＩの動向）エネルギー価格の上昇で総合指数が再加速 
米国のＣＰＩは、25年春以降に関税引き上げの影響などから上昇した後、いったん低下したもの
の、26年春先以降は再び加速している。総合指数（前年同月比）は25年 4月の+2.3％から同年 9
月に+3.0％まで上昇した後、26年2月には+2.4％まで低下した。しかし、中東情勢の緊迫化に伴う
エネルギー価格の上昇を背景に、3月には+3.3％と再加速し、9月には+3.7％まで上昇した（前掲図
表 1） 。一方、コア指数（食料品・エネルギーを除く）は25年 7～8月の+3.1％から 26年 8月には
+2.4％まで低下した後、9月は+2.6％と小幅に加速したものの、 総合指数との乖離は拡大している。 
ＣＰＩの内訳をみると、 エネルギー価格は26
年 2 月の前年同月比 +0.5％から 3 月には
+12.5％へ急伸し、9月には+20.4％まで上昇す
るなど、総合指数の再加速を主導している（図
表2） 。一方、コア財価格は25年春以降、関税
引き上げの影響などからプラスに転じ、同年8
～9月には+1.5％まで上昇したものの、26年 9
月には+0.7％まで鈍化した。また、コアサービ
ス価格のうち、住居費の伸びは 23年以降大幅
に鈍化したものの、26年春先に再び加速し、足
元でも+3％台前半で下げ渋っている。一方、住
居費を除くサービスも春先に伸びが加速した後、足元では+3％程度で推移している。 
このように、足元のインフレ再加速は主にエネルギー価格の上昇によるものであり、コア財価格
の伸びは鈍化しているほか、サービス価格の上昇圧力はピーク時から緩和したものの、足元では根
強さがみられる。

### Page 3

こうした中、ニューヨーク連銀の試算では、関税による消費財価格上昇率への寄与度は 26年後
半にマイナスに転じ、27年１月には▲0.7％ポイントとなる見通しである（前掲図表３） 。これは、
25年の大幅な関税引き上げによる価格上昇が前年比の比較対象から外れるためである。もっとも、
関税による物価水準の押し上げ効果は、関税率の引き下げなどから縮小するものの、消失するわけ
ではない。 
このように、既存関税によるインフレ押し上げ効果は今後減衰すると見込まれる。ただし、トラ
ンプ政権は通商法301条に基づく追加関税措置を進めており、今後の通商政策次第では、インフレ
率が再び押し上げられる可能性がある。

（エネルギー価格の影響）中東情勢の緊迫化がインフレを押し上げ 
エネルギー価格は、26年 2月末に始まったイラン戦争を背景に大幅に上昇している。原油先物価
格（ＷＴＩ）は25年末にかけて1バレル60ドル前後まで低下していたが、イラン戦争の開始を受
けて急騰し、26年春先には一時110ドルを上回った（図表5） 。その後、いったん70ドル前後まで
下落したものの、足元では再び上昇しており、10月 22日には 99.5ドルと高水準で推移している。

（注）関税による非石油系消費財価格の前年比上昇率への寄与度。直接効果は関税対象品目への
価格転嫁、競合価格効果は競合企業の価格調整、コスト上昇効果は輸入中間財などの費用増加を
通じた影響。2026年3月以降は予測値。
（資料）ニューヨーク連銀「Liberty Street Economics」（2026年10月6日）よりニッセイ基礎研究所作成

### Page 4

（ＡＩ関連投資の影響）電力需要の拡大が電力料金の上昇圧力に 
ＡＩ関連投資の拡大に伴うデータセンターの電力需要増加も、電力料金の上昇要因として注目さ
れている。米国の電力料金（消費者物価指数、前年同月比）は、22年 8月に+15.8％まで上昇した
後、23年末には+3％台まで鈍化したものの、24
年以降はプラス圏で推移し、25年後半には+6％
台まで加速した（図表6）。その後は伸びが鈍化
したものの、26年9月には＋4.4％と前月の＋
3.8％から小幅に加速した。 
電力料金の上昇には、ＡＩ関連投資の拡大に
伴うデータセンターの電力需要増加が影響して
いる可能性がある。データセンターは大量の電
力を安定的に消費するため、需要の急増に対し
て発電設備や送配電網の整備が追いつかない場
合には、電力需給の逼迫や設備投資費用の増加
を通じて電力料金の上昇圧力となる。また、電力インフラの整備には長い期間を要することから、
こうした影響が長期化する可能性もある。 
もっとも、電力料金は天然ガスなどの燃料価格や送配電設備の更新費用、地域ごとの料金規制な
ど複数の要因によって決定される。このため、足元の電力料金上昇のうち、ＡＩ関連投資による押
し上げ効果がどの程度を占めるかを特定することは難しい。今後もＡＩ関連投資の拡大が見込まれ
る中、電力需要の増加と供給能力の拡充の動向が、電力料金を通じたインフレ圧力を左右する要因
として注目される。

### Page 5

（インフレ見通し）26年はエネルギー高で加速、27年は鈍化へ 
こうした中、中長期のインフレ期待が比較的安定していることも踏まえ、今後のインフレ率は、
26年中はエネルギー価格の上昇によって高止まりするものの、27年には関税による物価押し上げ
効果の剥落などから鈍化すると予想する。 
関税については、25年春以降の関税引き上げに伴う価格転嫁が一巡するほか、実効関税率がピー
ク時から低下していることもあって、今後の物価押し上げ効果は減衰するとみられる。一方、エネ
ルギー価格は中東情勢の緊迫化を背景に当面高止まりする可能性があるものの、27年には原油価格
の上昇一服や前年のベース効果の剥落から、 総合ＣＰＩへの押し上げ効果が縮小すると見込まれる。
また、ＡＩ関連投資の拡大に伴う電力需要の増加は、引き続き電力料金の上昇圧力となる可能性が
あるものの、ＣＰＩ全体への影響は限定的とみられる。 
当研究所では、総合ＣＰＩ上昇率（前年比）が25年の+2.6％から26年には+3.4％へ加速する一
方、27年には+2.5％へ鈍化すると予想する。もっとも、27年もＣＰＩ上昇率は2％台半ばで推移す
る見通しであり、インフレ圧力の完全な沈静化にはなお時間を要するとみられる。

（今後の注目点）エネルギー価格や追加関税などが上振れリスク 
今後のインフレ動向を巡っては、基本シナリオに対する上振れリスクとして、エネルギー価格の
一段の上昇や追加関税措置などが挙げられる。
